- **Block B1 outcome (July 9–10, `f74ee29` + `605961a`, client sibling `13f48c3`):** all three sub-fixes landed as **fix-to-conform against LOCKED PRD spec** (§2.2 / §2.8 / §12.7 / §12.8) — **no PRD redline**. Device-tested green. api-server **1162 pass / 0 fail / 2 skip**; mobile **757 / 0**. Key reframe established during the amend (carried into B2, and **shipped there**): **the grocery line is two-part — `purchase size (need quantity)`** (e.g. "5 lb bag flour (1.5 cups)", "1 bottle cardamom (2 tbsp)"). The **need quantity is decision-support data** — it exists so the user can judge "do I need to buy this, or do I have enough?" — so it must stay **fine-grained and must NOT be rounded toward a purchasable amount**. The **purchase size** is the rounded-to-a-buyable-pack number and is **B2's** deliverable. B1 owns the honest need number; B2 owns the pack number and composes the line.
- **Root cause (Phase 0 diagnosis, July 8) — five distinct sub-causes across two subsystems:**
  - **25-1 · Purchase-size math** ("1 head Garlic · 30 clove", "2 lemons · 5.5 each") — **✅ FIXED, Block B2 (`2798db0`).** *Original diagnosis:* no unit-conversion table between purchase units and recipe sub-units. `ingredientPurchaseDefaults` gave garlic `{purchaseUnit:"head", purchaseQuantity:1, purchaseDisplay:"1 head"}`; `buildDeterministicOutputItem` rendered purchase size next to requested recipe qty with **no reconciliation**; the AI gap-fill saw only canonical-name + requested qty, with **no head↔clove equivalence**. **RULED (D-WS7-197): build the FULLER table, not an MVP subset** — Hans, July 9: "at this point in the build we should be working toward a fully complete product." **As built:** a single shared `ingredientConversions.ts` (**absorbing and deleting** `ingredientPurchaseDefaults.ts` — one source of truth, not two tables) carrying `gramsPerCup` / `gramsPerEach` / `subUnit{parent,perParent}` / purchase fields / `source` / `confidence`, seeded onto `Ingredient.conversionRef Json?` via migration + backfill. **Head↔clove now scales:** 1 head + 3 cloves → 13 cloves need → **"2 heads garlic"** (⌈13/10⌉); 30 cloves → **"3 heads garlic (30 cloves)"**. **Two consumers, one table** — grocery purchase-sizing *and* the macro gram-conversion (which had been an AI guess; see `88b212d`). **Invariant:** **grocery READS the table; only the macro path WRITES to it** (CC's Phase 3 pushback, ratified — a second AI conversion call on the grocery hot path would have been redundant *and* would have handed grocery a write path into the shared catalog). **Cross-ref BUG-031** (both its angles were real; fixed in the same commit).
  - **25-2 · Raw fractional "each"** (0.75 onion, 2.5 onion, 5.5 lemon) — **✅ FIXED (`f74ee29`, ⅛-ladder amend `605961a`).** Root cause confirmed: discrete counts never `Math.ceil`'d; `consolidatePlanIngredients` multiplies scaled quantities → decimals (`groceryList.ts:356`, accumulated `:401`) and nothing rounded them. **(Prompt-citation correction, §27:** the original `groceryListAI.ts:152-154` cite was wrong — that's the gap-fill write-back loop; `formatQuantityDisplay` is *client-side* at `grocery.ts:152-154`. The fix correctly belongs **server-side**.) **Fix as built:** a single `roundNeedQuantity` sweep over all entries at the end of `consolidatePlanIngredients` (flows to persisted quantity, AI input, and wire in one seam). **Discrete units** (each/clove/slice/can/jar/bottle/bunch/head/piece + unknown/empty — conservative) **ceil to whole**: 0.75 → 1, 5.5 → 6, 3.75 → 4. **Measured units** (cup/tbsp/tsp/oz/lb/g/kg/ml/l/pinch/quart/gallon + plurals) **round UP on the ⅛ ladder** `[0, ⅛, ¼, ⅓, ⅜, ½, ⅝, ⅔, ¾, ⅞, 1]`: 1.1 → 1⅛, 1.43 → 1½, 1.3 → 1⅓, 1.7 → 1¾; on-ladder values not inflated. **Ladder-granularity ruling:** PRD §2.8 permits "nearest ⅛ **or** a clean fraction (¼/⅓/½/⅔/¾)" — an initial ¼-ladder build was **deliberately reframed to ⅛** because the need quantity is decision-support data where granularity matters (Hans: "¼ cup extra store-bought tomato sauce isn't a big deal, but ¼ extra flour in a dumpling would be"). Boundary tests pin that round-**up** holds where thirds sit between eighths (1.34 → 1⅜ *not* 1⅓; 1.68 → 1¾ *not* 1⅔). **⚠️ B2 later found this sweep was BYPASSED on the AI merge path** — see BUG-031.
  - **25-3 · Recurring-item unit** ("bananas · 1 each" → "1 bunch") — **✅ FIXED (`f74ee29`, defaults extended in `605961a`).** Root cause confirmed: synthetic recurring entries hardcoded `quantity:1, unit:"each"`, `purchaseUnit/Quantity/Display: null`, never consulting `ingredientPurchaseDefaults` (`groceryList.ts:463-479`). **Fix as built:** `lookupPurchaseDefault(norm)` wired at the synthetic-entry construction — on hit, set unit/quantity/purchase* from the default; on miss, fall back to today's each/1/null. **Defaults table extended:** `banana`/`bananas`, `scallions`, `green onions`, `kale` → `{bunch, 1, "1 bunch"}`. **`spinach` deliberately HELD** — bagged-baby vs bunched-mature is genuinely ambiguous, and a default that's wrong half the time is worse than "each" (standing principle for this table: only add unambiguous entries).
  - **25-4 · Cross-sourcing** (lemon juice from lemons on the list): **NET-NEW LOGIC — no derivation/substitution layer exists.** Consolidation buckets on `(canonical, unit)` only (`groceryList.ts:263-485`); no ingredient→derived-form graph, no ratio table, no post-AI merge. **→ DEFERRED as D-WS7-196** (not an MVP bug-fix; building it = a substitution engine). *Unchanged — still deferred, NOT in B2 scope.*
  - **25-5 · Staples detection** ("Kosher salt" leaks as buy-item) — **✅ FIXED (`f74ee29`).** Root cause confirmed: exact-string match `UNIVERSAL_STAPLE_KEYS.has(normalizeIngredientName(canonical))` at BOTH assignment sites (`groceryList.ts:367` and the synthetic path `:470`); normalization only lowercases/collapses-whitespace/strips-articles (`groceryNormalization.ts:6-11`), so "kosher salt" ≠ "salt"; the seed row's `aliases:["salt"]` feeds ingredient *search*, never the staple check (confirmed: no alias consultation at either site). **Fix as built:** `STAPLE_VARIANT_TO_BASE` **exact-string** map + exported `baseStapleName()` (`groceryStaples.ts`), applied before the `.has()` check at both seams. **Exact-string, NOT substring** — this eliminates the false-positive class by construction. Families mapped: **salt** (kosher/sea/table/coarse/coarse-sea/flaky/flaky-sea/fine/fine-sea/kosher-sea), **black pepper** (cracked/ground/freshly-ground/fresh-ground black pepper, cracked/ground pepper, black peppercorns), **olive oil** (extra-virgin/extra virgin/virgin/light olive oil, evoo). **Deliberately NOT mapped:** generic `flour` (bread/almond/all-purpose risk) and bare `pepper` (bell/red/white/chili). **Must-NOT-match cases pinned RED-proof in tests:** `salted butter`, `garlic salt`, `celery salt`, `onion salt`, `seasoned salt`, `bell pepper`, `white pepper`, `red pepper flakes` — all absent from the exact map, never swept in. No stored `canonicalName`/`displayName` mutated — the user still sees "Kosher salt," it just renders greyed/unselected per §2.2/§12.7 [LOCKED].
  - **Companion client work (`13f48c3`, fraction-glyph block):** B1's fine-grained ⅛ values would have rendered as ugly decimals ("1.3333"), so a client glyph pass shipped alongside. **Two entry points, shared internals** (`lib/format/quantity.ts`): **`formatNeedGlyph`** for grocery — ε-exact (0.02) glyph match over eighths ∪ ⅓/⅔, **decimal fallback** for off-ladder values (an off-ladder value is AI-merge residue and should show verbatim rather than be faked to a glyph); **`formatQuantity`** extended with a **thirds** pass for meal-detail + cook-mode — it already glyphed eighths but **pre-rounded to the nearest ⅛, mangling ⅓ → ⅜ and ⅔ → ⅝** (a live wrong-render bug, now strictly corrected), keeping its glyph-always approximate behavior otherwise (1.9 → 1⅞). **Critical display-only constraint:** `quantityAmount` seeds the inline editor and is `parseFloat`'d on persist, so the glyph is applied **at render** (`displayQty`) and `quantityAmount` is never overwritten — edit round-trip intact (device-verified).
  - **AI merge-path prompt fix (rides the PREFS `0b7ab9e` commit, `aiPrompts.ts` rule 2):** the unit-mismatch merge rule explicitly told the model to "round … up to a purchase size" — **directly contradicting the fine-grained-need-quantity reframe**. Surgically edited: merge logic (merge-to-one-item, larger unit, OR flags, `notes`, `wasAiInferred`) untouched; only the rounding clause changed to *"Combine quantities accurately into the fine-grained total need quantity … Preserve that fine-grained total — do NOT round it toward a purchasable amount (purchase sizing is handled separately)."* Prompt-body change → version bump on reseed (`grocery.generate_list`).



### BUG-026 — "Kiwi needs a few specifics" clarify sheet is unusable (keyboard/scroll UX) ✅ FIXED (`ee8e306`)
- **Surface:** Mobile grocery clarify-items sheet ("Which one did you mean?").
- **Symptom (was):** Typing into the "Other…" free-text field was impossible — the keyboard covered/collapsed the sheet; scrolling didn't work. The clarify functionality itself worked; the sheet needed a keyboard-avoiding, scrollable container.
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED July 9, 2026 (`ee8e306`, WS7-8b Block C — local, pushed with the block) — device-tested green ("Other…" reachable with keyboard up; sheet scrolls on small screen / long option list).
- **Priority:** was P2.
- **Target:** WS7-8b **Block C** (mobile grocery screen) — shared `[id].tsx` with BUG-027.
- **Root cause (VERIFIED, Phase 0):** clarify sheet was a bare RN `Modal` → plain `View` backdrop/sheet, no `KeyboardAvoidingView`, no scroll container (`grocery-list/[id].tsx:1006-1103`); the autoFocus "Other…" `TextInput` (`:1064`) sat in a static non-scrollable `View` → keyboard overlaid it, couldn't scroll into view.
- **Fix (as built):** extracted the inline sheet to a new presentational **`ClarifySheetView.tsx`** (the codebase's established container/view split — mirrors `DishChooserSheetView` / `PrepWeekView`), wrapping `KeyboardAvoidingView` (`behavior` padding/height) + `ScrollView` (`keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"`, capped `maxHeight:90%` + `flexShrink` so overflow scrolls); `Keyboard.dismiss()` in `closeClarify`. All state/writes/clarify-logic/resolve-write/ambiguity flow stayed in the screen via props (extraction is presentational-only — §27 verified state didn't move). `[id].tsx` shed the now-unused `Modal`/`Platform`/`ScrollView`/`KeyboardAvoidingView` imports + ~17 clarify styles. **Judgment call ratified:** the extraction is structurally bigger than an in-place edit but was the only way to satisfy the prompt's render-test requirement (the test glob excludes `app/`) AND it's the house pattern — accepted. +4 render/prop tests (`ClarifySheetView.test.ts`, `.test.ts` in-glob). No PRD redline (§12.9 [LOCKED] already spec's the list mutable; layout-only fix).

### BUG-027 — Grocery "Add an item" predictive search spins and never resolves ✅ FIXED (`ee8e306`)
- **Surface:** Grocery list add-item text box (predictive search).
- **Symptom (was):** The box appeared predictive-search enabled but spun forever; suggestions never loaded.
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED July 9, 2026 (`ee8e306`, WS7-8b Block C — local, pushed with the block) — device-tested green (spinner stops within ~4s on the slow/zero-hit path; raw-text submit still available).
- **Priority:** was P2.
- **Target:** WS7-8b **Block C** (mobile grocery screen) — shared `[id].tsx` with BUG-026.
- **Root cause (VERIFIED, Phase 0):** endpoint IS implemented + tested (`GET /grocery-items/lookup`, `groceryLists.ts:578-643`) — a 502 CANNOT cause infinite spin (the effect's `.finally` clears the spinner on any *settled* response). Actual mechanism: the request never *settled*. The debounce effect (`[id].tsx:141-165`) used only a `cancelled` boolean (stale-response guard) that never aborted the in-flight fetch; `lookupGroceryItemCandidates` (`grocery.ts:275-286`) took no `signal` and `apiClient` (`builder.ts:15-17`) imposes no client-side timeout → a hung zero-hit AI fallback (`categorizeGroceryItem`, Haiku) left the promise pending forever, `.finally` never ran.
- **Fix (as built):** `AbortController` + **4000ms** `LOOKUP_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` in the effect, signal threaded through `lookupGroceryItemCandidates` (`opts.signal`, optional — no wire-shape change) to `apiClient`, abort-on-teardown. `apiClient` already wraps an `AbortError` into `ApiNetworkError` (`client.ts:159-166`) so the abort lands in `.catch` → `.finally` clears the spinner (the mechanism that makes the fix work — test-pinned). Mirror of D-WS7-080's wizard AbortController (shorter timeout appropriate to type-ahead vs plan-activate). **No non-AI fallback added** (ruled — raw-text Extras submit already covers the timed-out case; keeping scope tight). +2 tests (signal-forwarded-to-fetch; timeout-abort surfaces as a settled `ApiNetworkError`/`AbortError`). **§27 residual (unchanged):** *why* the fallback hangs on a specific device needs a live repro; the fix bounds the spin regardless of device cause — the device-side AI-reachability check is a separate diagnostic, not a Block C blocker.

### BUG-024 — Week Prep: manually checking every step in a phase doesn't auto-advance ✅ FIXED (`6227c6e`)
- **Surface:** Week Prep / phase progression (`PrepWeekScreen` / `PrepWeekView`).
- **Symptom (was):** When the user hand-checked ALL of a phase's checkboxes, the screen did nothing — only the "Mark all complete" button advanced. Hans-specified intended behavior (July 5): auto-advance to the next phase + intermediate toast "Done with X, moving to Y".
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED July 9, 2026 (`6227c6e`, local — pushed with the block) — device-tested green (non-last phase full-check → auto-advance + intermediate toast; last-phase full-check → terminal "Woohoo!", no double-advance, no advance-past-end; empty/vacuous phase 0 does NOT auto-skip on entry).
- **Priority:** was P2.
- **Target:** WS7-8b **Block A** (Week-Prep). **PRD §13.4.2 redline addendum QUEUED** (auto-advance-on-full-check behavior) — lands at WS7-8b close per §8, NOT applied this block.
- **Cross-ref:** BUG-020, D-WS7-188.
- **Root cause (VERIFIED, Phase 0):** two independent trigger paths existed; only the button path advanced. Per-step toggle called `toggle(stepKey, nextChecked)` and nothing else — never inspected phase completion. The VM already computed `phase.allDone` (`prepWeekModel.ts:190`) but nothing consumed it after a per-step toggle.
- **Fix (as built):** new pure `prepAutoAdvance.ts` guard (edge gate + idempotency latch) + a `PrepWeekScreen` `useEffect` on `vm.phases[phaseIndex].allDone`; single `commitAdvance(completedIndex)` applier both the effect and the button `after` route through. **Double-advance guard = two orthogonal mechanisms:** (1) **edge gate** (`isPrepAllDoneEdge`) fires only on a false→true flip → stops auto-advance on mount, on vacuously-empty phases, and on resumed-already-complete phases (none are edges); (2) **idempotency latch** (`requestPrepAdvance`/`releasePrepAdvance`) latches the completed index so whichever of {effect, button `.then`} arrives first advances and the other no-ops; the pointer move is absolute (`from+1`) so even a bypassed latch can't skip a phase; leaving `allDone` releases the latch (uncheck→recheck re-advances). Intermediate toast is a new VM-plumbed render in `PrepWeekView.tsx` (terminal "Woohoo!" untouched; terminal wins if both ever set). **§27:** the pure test simulates the container's real call interleavings and counts advances — button-then-effect + effect-then-button both → `advances===1`, pointer +1 never +2; last-phase both channels → `finishes===1` no double terminal toast; mount-on-empty → `advances===0`; re-completion after uncheck → advances again. Suite 726→745 (+19), tsc clean.
- **Judgment call logged (behavior delta):** the all-done effect fires on the OPTIMISTIC write, so a "Mark all complete" server write that later fails now advances-then-reverts (banner shows) where the button previously advanced only on `.then` success. Ruled the right call (consistency with the inherently-optimistic checkbox path); only manifests on a failed server write. **Guard-seam:** extracted to a pure module rather than a container render test (codebase has zero container tests; PrepWeekScreen isn't DI/ESM-mock-wired under `node:test`) — a full PrepWeekScreen render harness offered by CC, declined for now (candidate WS9 test-coverage item).

### BUG-029 — Preferences screen: "Could not save preferences" (strict-schema unknown-key rejection, silent 400) ✅ FIXED (`0b7ab9e`)
- **Surface:** Mobile Profile → Preferences (`preferences.tsx`) → `PATCH /me/preferences`.
- **Symptom (was):** editing any preference and saving showed "Couldn't save your preferences. Please try again." — with **zero output in the api-server terminal.** The silence was the diagnostic: a 500 would have logged, a network failure would have looked different. Surfaced in Cookbook Phase B device testing, July 10, 2026.
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED July 10, 2026 (`0b7ab9e`) — same session it surfaced.
- **Priority:** was P1 (a core screen couldn't save; blocked commit).
- **Root cause (TRACED, not hypothesized — §27):** Cookbook Phase B **Block 4** added `weeklyPacingDefault` to the **mobile** `UserPreferencesSchema` (`lib/api/me.ts`) so the wizard could hydrate pacing. `toFormState` seeds the edit buffer via `...p` (spread of the `getPreferences()` result), so the key entered `form` **at runtime** — **TypeScript never flagged it**, because the declared `UserPreferencesData` return type doesn't include it. `handleSave` then PATCHed the whole form (minus only `wantsLeftovers`). The server allow-list (`me.ts:165-192`) is **`.strict()`** → **rejects unknown keys** → 400. And the `!parsed.success` branch returned 400 **with no logger call** (only the 500 catch path had one) → device error, silent terminal. Confirming detail: `difficultyDefault` is *also* server-returned but stays stripped (still undeclared mobile-side) — which is precisely why only this one key leaked.
- **Fix (`0b7ab9e`), two parts:** **(a)** `toFormState` destructures server-only fields out, so `form` stays a true `UserPreferencesData`. This is the **class** fix — chosen over the minimal option (stripping this one key in `handleSave`, mirroring the `wantsLeftovers` precedent) because the minimal fix would leave the next read-schema addition free to reintroduce the same break. **(b)** The Zod-rejection branch **now logs** the validation error, so the next strict-schema mismatch is a one-line terminal read instead of a device-test mystery. Regression-tested both sides (server rejects a server-only key; `toFormState` emits none).
- **Lesson (standing trap):** a `.strict()` server allow-list plus a client that round-trips its **read** shape back into a **write** is a latent break. Any field added to the read schema that isn't in the write allow-list will silently kill saves — and TypeScript won't catch it, because the leak happens via runtime spread against an under-declared return type. The `toFormState` guard is the structural defense; the 400-logging is the detection.
- **Cross-ref:** D-WS7-035 (the per-run override layer whose hydration work introduced `weeklyPacingDefault` to the read schema).

### BUG-028 — USDA `/foods/search` 400 on slash-in-name aborts the backfill dry-run ✅ FIXED (`897ab56`)
- **Surface:** USDA FDC client (`fdcClient.searchFoods`) + backfill script (`ws7-8b-usda-backfill.ts`), WS7-8b USDA integration arc.
- **Symptom (was):** the Block 2 backfill dry-run aborted on the *first* ingredient, `80/20 ground beef`, with a USDA 400. A live probe confirmed `80/20 ground beef` → 400 but `ground beef` → 200 (quota fine, `rateLimitRemaining` 3598 — genuine 400 bad-request, not a 429). USDA's query parser rejects the `80/20` slash-between-digits token even when properly percent-encoded. Compounded by Block 2's deliberate conservative choice to throw `BackfillAbortError` on *any* non-ok search — so one deterministic-400 name aborted the whole 383-row run.
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED July 6, 2026 (`897ab56`, local — pushed with the block) — two coupled fixes. **Fix A — query sanitization:** new exported `sanitizeUsdaQuery` in `fdcClient.searchFoods` spaces out every non-`\p{L}`/`\p{N}`/whitespace/apostrophe/hyphen char (so `/ \ % # &` become spaces), collapses whitespace, trims; empty-result fallback returns the original query (all-punctuation guard); digits preserved so `80/20` ≠ `90/10` don't collide. The DB ingredient name is never touched — only the outbound USDA query string. **Fix B — 4xx-vs-transient split** in `decideForIngredient`: deterministic 4xx (400–499, incl. 403/404/422) → per-row MISS with a distinct `usda_unsearchable_<status>` reason, run continues; transient (429 `rate_limited`, 5xx, network, timeout, malformed) → keeps the `BackfillAbortError` abort. 429 is structurally safe — it surfaces as its own `rate_limited` reason, never `http_error`, so the 4xx→MISS rule can't catch it.
- **Priority:** was P1 (blocked the USDA backfill; blocked WS7-8b USDA arc).
- **Root cause (VERIFIED):** live probe — `80/20 ground beef` → 400, `ground beef` → 200, identical `dataType`/auth, quota fine. USDA's parser chokes on the slash-between-digits token even percent-encoded; a real USDA API quirk, not a Kiwi encoding bug.
- **Ratification:** tests 1108→1121 (+13), tsc clean. **Live dry-run ran clean** — 383/383 processed (AUTO 218 · AI_PICK 78 · MISS 87), **zero** `usda_unsearchable_*` rows (the sanitizer rescued every name that would have 4xx'd, so Fix B's safety net never had to fire), full CSV written, no abort. **`--apply` landed clean:** 296 matches + 87 miss-markers written, 0 skipped, **0 catalog rows null after apply** (full coverage — closes the Block 1 "383 null rows never revisited" concern). Judgment call ratified by Hans: Fix B generalized strict-400 → all-4xx (404→MISS test pins it).
- **Design note:** CC widened deterministic-MISS from strict-400 to all 4xx (400–499). Ratified keep — any non-429 4xx on a specific name is deterministic (re-running fails identically); `--retry-misses` is the backstop for the theoretical retryable-4xx case.
- **Cross-ref:** D-WS7-192 (transient-abort has no resume-from-offset — *partially* addressed here: the deterministic-400 abort is gone, but the transient-abort-no-resume point stands).

### BUG-017 — Categorizer `inferCategory` false-positives (keyword-rule gaps) ✅ FIXED (`88fa52a`) + backfill APPLIED
- **Surface:** Ingredient categorization (`inferCategory`, `ingredientResolve.ts`) at ingredient create → Week Prep phase grouping + grocery categorization.
- **Symptom (was):** fish sauce → Protein; peanut butter → Dairy; corn tortillas → Produce; y→ies plural gaps (blueberries/strawberries → Pantry fallback); no-keyword misses (edamame, pizza dough, bean sprouts).
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED July 5, 2026 (`88fa52a`) — new Pantry condiments/nut-butters precedence rule + Bakery tortilla/dough override + y→ies plural matching + new keywords; 14 test pins incl. must-not-move regression block (suite 1031→1045); backfill script generalized to full-sweep (DRY-RUN default). Hans applied: 7 rows corrected (incl. ratified judgment rows `large eggs`→Dairy, `edamame`→Produce), post-apply sweep 0 drift. Device test passed.
- **Root cause (VERIFIED, Phase 0):** (i) single-word keyword `\b` matches firing inside compound names (fish/butter/corn); (ii) plural regex only handled +s/+es, missing y→ies; (iii) missing keywords. Only ONE persistence path exists (`resolveIngredients` upsert create, write-once) — rule fix + catalog backfill covered the whole surface. USDA integration ABSENT from codebase (env key had zero references) → Hans ruled it IN scope as the next arc (PRD §11.8/§11.9), rules hardened first by design.
- **Cross-ref:** BUG-012 (the narrow precursor rule + reversed no-backfill lesson); Hans rulings July 5 (9-category vocabulary kept; jar/can condiments → Pantry; coconut milk stays Canned).

- **Surface:** Week Prep generation — `prep.narrate_steps` / `PrepNarrationResultSchema`.

- **Priority:** was P1 (blocked big-plan generation).
- **Root cause (VERIFIED):** D-WS7-151's "one giant blend step" collapse × a big plan — per-step cap at `prepNarration.ts:76` + `prepWeek.ts:72`.
- **Fix (Hans-ruled, Option B):** per-dish blend split — `seasonings_dry#dish#<dishId>`, sauces-sibling symmetry, regenerate/orphan-prune, NO migration. Option A (raise the cap) DECLINED. Full rule: **D-WS7-187**. Residual truncation axis (whole-call maxTokens) surfaced separately as BUG-022.

### BUG-020 — No partial phase-advance in Prep the Week ✅ FIXED (`0c5d60e`)
- **Surface:** Week Prep phase navigation — footer actions + progress bar.
- **Symptom:** The only advance was "Done with phase ✓," which force-marked ALL unchecked steps complete — no way to skip past uncut tomatoes without falsely checking them off. Partial prep impossible.

Progress bar: past phases with any unchecked step render a distinct partial state (sage @0.45 opacity) — ruling **D-WS7-188**; `CookFooter` gained an optional back-compat `secondaryActions` prop, `ProgressSegments` an optional `partialIndices` prop (CookSessionView regression-tested identical). Server semantics deliberately untouched: meals with unchecked steps stay not-prepped and the Cook gate prompts — desired partial-prep behavior (Hans-confirmed). **PRD §13.4.2 redline QUEUED** (replaces the two-button spec).
- **Cross-ref:** BUG-021 (resume-at-phase-0, logged from this arc's Phase 0); D-WS7-184 (server rollup — untouched here, Block 2's job).

### BUG-022 — Prep narration truncates at the 4096 default maxTokens on heavy plans ✅ FIXED (`15f2396`)
- **Surface:** `prep.narrate_steps` call site (`cooking.ts`) / `runAICall` default.
- **Symptom:** Heavy plan (48.8K input tokens) → forced tool_use JSON hit the 4096 default output cap mid-`steps` array → parsed to `{}` → `steps: Required` → 502. Telltale: `outputTokens: 8192` = 4096 × 2 retry attempts, ~105s latency (two full truncated generations).
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED July 4, 2026 (`15f2396`).
- **Priority:** was P1 (blocked the BUG-020 device test on heavy plans).
- **Fix:** `PREP_NARRATION_MAX_TOKENS = 16384` passed at the call site (named-constant pattern, matches `WIZARD_EXPAND_PER_MEAL_MAX_TOKENS`); regression test asserts the raised ceiling. Residual pathological headroom (→ chunked narration) deferred as **D-WS7-189**.

### BUG-011 — Dense blend paragraph ✅ FIXED (`da857bc`)
- **Surface:** Week Prep / Screen 3 — collapsed `seasonings_dry` blend step.
- **Symptom:** All per-dish measures render as one run-on prose block instead of one line per dish.
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED July 4, 2026 (`da857bc`, WS7-8b Block 1) — prompt-only `\n` fix; device-verified (measures on separate lines) on the BUG-016 6-meal retest. Note: the collapsed blend step itself was subsequently split per-dish (BUG-016 / D-WS7-187); the `\n` formatting survives inside each per-dish step.
- **Priority:** P2 (readability on a primary screen; no data risk).
- **Target:** WS7-8b Block 1.
- **Root cause (VERIFIED, CC Phase 0):** `instructions` is one string (`prepNarration.ts:76`); the v6 prompt tells the narrator to comma-join measures (`aiPrompts.ts:1147`). The view already honors `\n` (lossless reconstruct in `quantityHighlight.ts:38-56`; RN `<Text>` with no `numberOfLines` cap, `PrepWeekView.tsx:133`).
- **Fix (ruled):** PROMPT-ONLY — emit `\n` between per-dish measures in the prompt body. No view change. Card stays one step / one checkbox (per-line checkboxes stay deferred). Tested via fresh-generate.

### BUG-012 — Spices land in Produce phase (garlic/onion powder mis-categorized) ✅ FIXED (`da857bc`)
- **Surface:** Ingredient categorization (`inferCategory` at ingredient-create) → surfaces in Week Prep phase grouping.
- **Symptom:** Garlic powder / onion powder appear under Produce instead of Pantry.
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED July 4, 2026 (`da857bc`, WS7-8b Block 1) — powder/granulated/dried Pantry rule + the 5-row spice backfill via `scripts/ws7-8b-b1-recategorize-ingredients.ts`; device-verified. **The row's original NO-backfill sub-call was REVERSED** (shared-catalog rows are reused, never re-inferred — create-time-only rules don't reach existing rows; canonical lesson in the deferred log's July 4 change-log row). Broader categorizer false-positives (fish sauce→Protein etc.) split out as BUG-017.
- **Priority:** P2.
- **Target:** WS7-8b Block 1.
- **Root cause (VERIFIED, CC Phase 0):** NOT `assignPhase` — it's `inferCategory`. The Produce rule's keywords include "onion"/"garlic" (`ingredientResolve.ts:91-93`), word-boundary matched (`:149-154`), so "garlic powder" matches "garlic" → Produce before the Pantry fallback; no spice/powder rule exists and Produce precedes others. Stored on `Ingredient.category` at create (`:237`), read verbatim by the loader (`prepWeekAggregation.ts:158-160`).
- **Fix (ruled):** add a Pantry rule keyed on **`powder` + `granulated` + `dried`** before Produce (Hans ruled include `dried` — routes dried thyme/oregano/basil → Pantry, correct/shelf-stable; CC verified zero fresh-produce collision). **NO backfill** — rule affects create-time only; existing rows intentionally left as-is; Hans tests by generating a fresh plan (§5, no real data to preserve). *(No companion deferred-decisions entry for a "decision" here beyond the no-backfill call, which is captured in the Block 1 close notes; the categorizer rule itself is a straight fix.)*

### BUG-014 — Combines-into-a-mix vs. standalone demotion rule ✅ FIXED (`da857bc`)
- **Surface:** Week Prep narration (`prep.narrate_steps`) — which single-ingredient measures stay in prep vs. get demoted.
- **Symptom:** A lone standalone measure that combines into nothing (oil → pan) is kept in prep, asking the user to pre-measure oil days ahead; meanwhile a measure that combines INTO a sauce/marinade/blend should stay. No structural signal reliably distinguished the two (`relevantSteps` alone fails when recipe prose is silent).
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED July 4, 2026 (`da857bc`, WS7-8b Block 1) — the D-WS7-185 rule shipped (`dishRole` signal, combines-into-mix=KEEP / standalone=DEMOTE, `isBlend` excluded); device-verified (lone oil marked/muted; combining measures not muted). The omit-from-render + server-exclude CONSUMPTION of `skipSuggested` is BUG-013 → Block 2.
- **Priority:** P2.
- **Target:** WS7-8b Block 1.
- **Root cause (VERIFIED, CC Phase 0):** the narrator saw `components`/`isBlend`/`relevantSteps`/`phase` but had no structural "which sauce does this belong to" signal; `isBlend` only fires for the collapsed seasonings blend, so a lone sauce ingredient isn't flagged, and `relevantSteps` is prose-dependent.
- **Fix (ruled):** the new **combines-into-a-mix vs. standalone demotion rule** — a single-ingredient measure that combines INTO a marinade/sauce/dressing/seasoning blend = **KEEP**; a standalone single measure combining into nothing (oil → pan) = **DEMOTE**. Reads the new `dishRole` signal (built this block, see BUG-016-adjacent assembly work); `isBlend` explicitly excluded from demotion (D-WS7-183 mixed-blend keep-loud stays dominant). **This new rule is a canonical behavioral rule → logged as D-WS7-185.**

### BUG-004 — "Add to plan" → WS7 stub + conditional display ✅ FIXED (`af27018`)
- **Surface:** Plan-instance Meal Detail screen — an "Add to Plan" button appeared even when the meal was already in a plan, and (on the Library path) tapping it landed on a "coming in WS7" stub.
- **Symptom:** (a) button shown in plan-context where it shouldn't appear; (b) the pick-existing-plan branch was a stub.
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED (`af27018`).
- **Priority:** was P2 (stub) + P3 (conditional display).
- **Root cause (VERIFIED, Surface A audit June 26):** the `onPickExistingPlan` callback (`meal/[id].tsx:495-505`) was an `Alert("Coming in WS7")` stub; the button rendered unconditionally. A real path (`addMealToPlan` → `POST /plans/:id/items`) already existed.
- **Fix:** (a) button gated to render only in Library context (`{!inPlanContext && …}`, `inPlanContext = !!(planId && planItemId)`); (b) `onPickExistingPlan` wired to `useApp().addMealToPlan(plan.id, meal.id)` with success/failure Alerts. Create-new-plan branch untouched. Mobile-only, one file.
- **Note (deferred):** add-to-plan from Library lands the meal slot=`dinner`, no specific day (existing `addMealToPlan` default); a day/slot picker is a possible later enhancement, not a bug.

### BUG-007 — Legacy "Save to my meal forever" / "Keep plan-only" button box on Meal Detail ✅ FIXED (`af27018`)
- **Surface:** Plan-instance Meal Detail (top of screen).
- **Symptom:** Dead legacy box with two buttons sat at the top of the screen.
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED (`af27018`) — pure deletion.
- **Priority:** was P2 (dead legacy UI on a primary screen).
- **Root cause (VERIFIED):** both handlers (`onSaveGlobally`/`onKeepPlanOnly`) were console.log + Alert no-ops; `bannerDismissed` state read only within the component. Confirmed dead UI.
- **Fix:** deleted the JSX block, both handlers, the `bannerDismissed` state, the `showBanner` computed value, and the three local styles; the old `hasOverride` const was repurposed into the shared `inPlanContext` gate (same expression). Grep-confirmed zero dangling references.

### BUG-003 — Persist canonical servings change (step↔ingredient consistency) ✅ FIXED (`a9d002e` + `dd8a4d3` + `80ab5b2`)
- **Surface:** Recipes/library → meal detail → servings stepper (canonical Save); plan-instance Meal Detail (override Save).
- **Symptom:** Stepper adjusted ingredient quantities live but there was no way to persist the change. Reframed (June 23-25, 2026) into a two-block feature: Block 1 (`52775f8`) = the structured-reference foundation (`amountRefs` sidecar keeping free-form step text consistent with structured quantities under scaling); Block 2 (this close) = the canonical Save itself.
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED — three sub-blocks on `ws7-in-progress`, all committed + pushed.
- **Priority:** was P2 (feature gap, not corruption).
- **Root cause:** a servings change scaled ingredient amounts at *render* only and was never persisted; the persistence path also risked baking in a permanent text-vs-quantity desync and (under a naive rescale-stored-literals approach) accumulated rounding drift across repeated rescales (4→8→6).
- **Resolution (Approach D — immutable anchor + scale-at-render):** a servings change writes ONLY the integer `servingsDefault`; stored authored quantities + `amountRefs` never move; all scaling is render-time `authoredQuantity × servingsDefault / authoredServingsDefault`. The `authoredServingsDefault` anchor (on Dish + Meal) is **immutable after create**, preserved across BOTH the scalar Save path AND rematerialize content edits. This eliminates 4→8→6 rescale drift, avoids wipe-recreate ref-destruction, and keeps servings changes on the scalar PATCH path so `amountRefs` survive.
  - **`a9d002e` (B2.1):** immutable `authoredServingsDefault` anchor added (Dish + Meal) + render-time divisor swap across 5 sites (Meal Detail, Cook Mode, grocery, prep). Foundation, no Save behavior.
  - **`dd8a4d3` (B2.2):** plan-instance servings Save gate — explicit Save, silent discard on back-out, Cook Now uses the saved value.
  - **`80ab5b2` (B2.3):** canonical servings Save (scalar promote via the `me.ts` scalar path, no `dishes[]`, drift-free) + Option-1 anchor-preservation guard in `rematerializeMeal` (recreated dishes inherit `meal.authoredServingsDefault`).
- **Product behavior:** plans are shared-live (read canonical records), so a canonical servings change propagates to un-overridden plan instances; overridden instances are pinned (ruled product behavior, PRD §2.5 — see D-WS7-175).
- **Device-tested:** green, including 4→8→6 drift-free and content-edit-holds-anchor.

### BUG-006 — Cook Mode renders unscaled ingredient amounts ✅ FIXED (`52775f8`)
- **Surface:** Cook Mode (per-step amount rendering) + meal-detail "Cook Now" launch.
- **Symptom:** Cook Mode rendered `amountRefs` at multiplier 1 (base servings), so a meal scaled to e.g. 6 servings on Meal Detail told the user base amounts in Cook Mode — Meal Detail and Cook Mode disagreed (the exact step↔ingredient desync BUG-003 exists to kill, surfacing on the cook screen). A second facet: meal-detail "Cook Now" dropped `planItemId`, so Cook Mode loaded no plan context and fell back to base amounts even after the fix.
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED (`52775f8`, folded into BUG-003 Block 1 commit).
- **Priority:** was P2 (consistency gap on a primary screen).
- **Root cause (VERIFIED):** (1) `CookSessionView` `StepText` called `buildAmountRefSegments(text, amountRefs, 1)` — hardcoded multiplier. (2) meal-detail Cook Now passed `{ mealId }` only (a stale "B2 temporary stub" handler), dropping `planItemId`/`planId`, so `useMeal(mealId, undefined)` resolved no `servingsOverride`.
- **Fix:** Cook Mode now scales `amountRefs` by `effectiveServings / authoredServingsDefault` (pure `resolveAmountMultiplier` with divide-by-zero guard → 1); plumbed `amountMultiplier` through `cook-session.tsx → CookSessionView → StepText`. Meal-detail Cook Now now passes plan context via a shared pure `buildCookSessionParams` (only when `planId` + `planItemId` both present — Library path stays `{ mealId }`), plus an `await` on the pending `setServingsForPlanItem` write before navigation (closes the adjust-then-immediately-cook race). dishId path = multiplier 1 (standalone dish, no override — the single-dish-of-an-overridden-plan-meal gap is unreachable in UI, logged closed in the deferred log). Meal Detail render untouched.
- **Device-tested:** scaled-plan-meal Cook Now (both card + meal-detail launch agree); Library cook (base amounts); the adjust-then-cook timing path.

### BUG-057 — Dish Builder edit mode silently FORKS: saving an edited dish creates a brand-new dish instead of updating it
- **Surface:** `app/dish-builder.tsx` — edit mode (entered from Dish Detail, or any edit affordance that routes into the dish builder with an existing `dishId`).
- **Symptom:** A user opens an existing dish, changes something, saves — and ends up with **two dishes**. The original is untouched; the edit lands as a new record. No error, no warning, no indication the edit went anywhere other than where the user expected.
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED — resolved in Block 3f-2 (`5360d03` → `e37048b`), independently VERIFIED in code August 5, 2026.** The status field below read OPEN for three days after the fix shipped. Logged August 2, 2026.
- ✅ **CONFLICT RESOLVED IN FAVOUR OF FIXED — read from code, not inferred.** Both candidate defects are gone: edit mode hydrates from the **server** (`useDish(dishId)` → `setForm(dishDetailToForm(sourceDish))`, `dish-builder.tsx:218-226`, with a header comment at `:116-118` recording the stub-path removal), and save **branches on the id** (`if (isEdit && form.id) { updateDish(id, …) /* PATCH /me/dishes/:id */; return }`, `:534-564`) with the unconditional `saveDish` POST now reached only on the create branch (`:567`). ⚠️ **So the three docs recording FIXED were right and this entry was the stale one — the opposite of the direction canon usually fails.** ⚠️ **Both candidate mechanisms (a) and (b) below turned out to be REAL and BOTH were fixed** — this was not an either/or after all, which is precisely why the entry was told not to pick a side by inference.
- ✅ **RESOLVED — the conflict flag below is kept as history. (Flagged August 5, 2026, doc-reconciliation session; settled August 5 by the 3f-4 Phase 0 grep, exactly as instructed.)** ⚠️ **STATUS WAS CONFLICTED — DO NOT ACT ON EITHER SIDE WITHOUT A GREP.** This entry reads OPEN. **`kiwi_ws9_plan.md`, `kiwi_ws9_screen_plan.md` and the deferred-log pointer all record BUG-057 ✅ FIXED in Block 3f-2** (`5360d03` → `e37048b`, device-confirmed, pushed), naming **two** defects — stub hydration **and** an unconditional create-on-save fork — which maps onto both candidate mechanisms below. ⚠️ **Neither side is adopted here.** Canon has been wrong in both directions this arc. **3f-4 Phase 0 owns the grep**; whichever way it resolves, correct the losing side rather than silently dropping it. ⚠️ **DEVICE-CONFIRMED August 2, 2026 (Hans), and the observed symptom is WORSE than this entry originally described.**
- **⚠️ DEVICE REPRO (Hans, August 2, 2026):** *"I navigated to subnav Recipes, then Dishes, then clicked into Dish Detail. Then I clicked Edit and it went to the manual dish creation page."* So the user-visible failure is not merely *"edits fork into a new dish"* — **Edit does not open the dish at all.** It lands on a blank creation form. A user cannot edit a dish today by any path.
- **⚠️ TWO CANDIDATE MECHANISMS, NOT YET DISCRIMINATED — DO NOT ASSUME:** (a) the diagnosed **stub hydration** (`dish-builder.tsx:113-120` seeds from `lib/stubs`, so the form renders empty even though `dishId` arrived); or (b) **Dish Detail's Edit navigates to `/dish-builder` with no `dishId` param at all**, in which case a creation form is the *correct* render and hydration is irrelevant. **Same symptom, different repair.** ⚠️ **This project's recurring failure is a plausible mechanism attached to a real symptom and adopted without checking — four logged instances.** **3f-2's Phase 0 must determine which before any fix is written.** If (b), the save-as-create half is a second, independent defect rather than the same one.
- **Priority:** **P2** (ship-soon). Not P1: it does not block a commit and corrupts nothing already on disk — but it silently multiplies the user's dish library and every edit is lost from the record the user thinks they edited.
- **Target:** **WS9 Block 3f-2** (builders sub-block — the block already opens this file for the restyle + Tags section, so the fix rides an edit that is happening anyway per §27.2).
- **Root cause (VERIFIED, CC Phase 0 August 2, 2026):** two independent halves, and **fixing either alone leaves the bug**:
  1. **Hydration is a stub.** Edit mode seeds the form from `lib/stubs` (`dish-builder.tsx:113-120`) rather than fetching the real dish — so the form never holds the true current record.
  2. **Save is unconditionally a create.** The save path posts a new dish (`dish-builder.tsx:490-491`); there is no update branch keyed on the presence of `dishId`.
- **⚠️ Why this stayed invisible:** the failure is **silent and success-shaped** — the save returns 200, the router navigates, and the user sees a dish that looks correct. It is the same failure class as the Block 3d Part 4 P1 (a swap that rejected into an uncaught promise) and the `plans.ts:793` comment that lied: **the system reports success for work it did not do.** ⚠️ **A green device test proves nothing here** — a tester who edits a dish and sees a dish would report a pass.
- **Fix direction (NOT yet ruled — scope at 3f-2 commissioning):** hydrate edit mode from the real dish (the meal builder's hydrate path is the precedent to check first per §27.2), and branch save on `dishId` to a real `PATCH /me/dishes/:id` — ⚠️ **which already exists** (`routes/me.ts:1409`, and it already supports independent ingredient-vs-step wiping). **The update endpoint is not the missing piece; the client never calls it.**
- **⚠️ Blast radius to check at build (§27):** how many forked duplicate dishes already exist for the test user, and whether any forked dish is linked into a meal via `MealDishLink` — a fork that happened mid-plan may have left a meal pointing at the *unedited* original.
- **Related:** D-WS7-050 (the same file's dead Side/Main + cuisine pickers, ruled out August 2 — see that entry); D-WS9-101 (Tags section, same file, same sub-block).

### BUG-001 — Servings adjustment lost on fast re-entry ✅ FIXED (`52775f8`)
- **Surface:** Meal Detail stepper (in-plan).
- **Symptom:** Adjust servings → back out → re-enter quickly → change not persisted (stale cache served on fast re-entry).
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED (`52775f8`).
- **Root cause (VERIFIED):** `setServingsForPlanItem` invalidated `["plans"]`/`["home"]`/`["groceries"]` but NOT `["meals","detail"]`; React Query served the 60s-fresh stale cache on fast re-entry → mount seeded `displayServings` from the stale value.
- **Fix:** `setServingsForPlanItem` now invalidates `["meals","detail"]`, mirroring the sibling `changeRecipeForPlanItem`; `queryClient` added to deps. Invalidation-only (the screen's local `setDisplayServings` covers in-session feel). Test added.

### BUG-002 — Edit-mode servings stepper persists count/ingredient mismatch (data corruption) ✅ FIXED (`52775f8`)
- **Surface:** Meal Detail → Edit mode → servings stepper → Apply Always.
- **Symptom:** Edit-mode stepper changed the servings number without rescaling ingredient quantities; Apply Always then persisted a count/amount mismatch on disk (canonical corruption).
- **Status:** ✅ FIXED (`52775f8`) — ruling B (remove edit-mode stepper).
- **Root cause (VERIFIED):** edit-mode ingredient rows are authored literal strings (no multiplier); the `MetaFields` servings stepper fed `servingsDefault` into `buildUpdateMealInput` while `dishes` carried unscaled authored quantities.
- **Fix:** `MetaFields` servings stepper gated **create-only** (`isEditMode = !!mealId`, threaded through both render sites); `buildUpdateMealInput` drops `servingsDefault` from the edit PATCH body; the keystone's edit-side seed reverted (with a "do NOT restore" comment — conscious rollback; the Meal-Detail half of the keystone stays). `buildUpdateMealInput` extracted to `lib/meal-builder-state.ts` and unit-tested (asserts `servingsDefault` ABSENT from the edit PATCH even with a non-default input — the corruption guard). Create builders keep `servingsDefault`.

### BUG-060 — Macro values render raw float sums: `49.000000001g` fat on the **Add Meals picker** ✅ FIXED — ⚠️ **heading corrected August 6, 2026; it read "on Meal Detail" for two days after the body was corrected, and Meal Detail was never affected**

- **Surface:** ⚠️ **CORRECTED August 4, 2026 — the original entry named the WRONG SCREEN, and the error was chat-Claude's.** The defect lived on the **Add Meals picker sheet** (`components/AddMealsSheet.tsx:169`), reached from a plan's "Add Meals" action — **not** Meal Detail. Hans confirmed the surface on re-test. ⚠️ **Meal Detail and fourteen other surfaces were ALREADY correct** and had been all along.
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED — commit `3b5c8e5`, `ws7-in-progress`, pushed** (`e37048b..3b5c8e5`, ref range read from Hans's push output). Device-verified on the picker.
- **Discovered:** August 4, 2026, WS9 Block 3f-3 device test — Hans's hands on the app, not any test. **Reproducing record: meal `458ad577-8c60-40c4-9b09-99a573857179`** (`GET /api/meals/458ad577-8c60-40c4-9b09-99a573857179` → 200, 561ms).
- **Priority:** **P2** (ship-soon). Cosmetic, loses no data, blocks no commit — but it is on a marquee screen and reads as unfinished software.
- **Symptom:** fat displayed as `49.000000001g`. **Classic IEEE-754 accumulation** from summing per-dish macro floats. Hans: *"The UI is supposed to filter that, but it might on the server side. it should only display 1 decimal."*
- **THE TARGET BEHAVIOR — ⚠️ AMENDED AT FIX TIME, AND THE AMENDMENT IS THE POINT.** The original spec said *one decimal place*. **That was withdrawn.** `formatMacro` already rounds grams to **integer** across fifteen shipped surfaces; imposing one decimal would have changed the display on all fifteen and rewritten a passing test contract — **to fix a defect that lived on one line.** ⚠️ **The one-decimal instruction had been written on the assumption that surfaces render raw floats, which Phase 0 disproved.** **Ruled: keep integer grams app-wide.** Macros are **AI-estimated** (D-WS9-082), and a decimal advertises precision the data does not have.
- ✅ **ROOT CAUSE VERIFIED — and it was candidate (a), on a surface nobody had named.** `lib/format/macros.ts` holds **`formatMacro`** (the rounding core, `String(Math.round(value))`) and **`formatMacroLine`**, and **fifteen surfaces already route through them.** Exactly one site bypassed both: `AddMealsSheet.tsx:169` built its line as a raw template literal — `` `${meal.caloriesPerServing} cal · ${meal.proteinGPerServing}g P · …` `` — so it rendered the unrounded float. **Fixed by routing that one call through `formatMacroLine`.** `formatMacro`'s contract and its existing tests were left untouched.
- ⚠️ **THE FIRST GREP COULD NOT HAVE FOUND IT, AND THAT IS THE DURABLE LESSON.** The Phase 0 sweep pattern was `toFixed|Math.round|formatMacro|formatNumber` — **none of which appear in a raw `${value}g` template literal.** The site was found by walking PRD §11.4's surface list, not by grepping. A second sweep for the **template-literal shape** (`${...}g` / `${...} cal`) across the whole mobile app then confirmed it is the only one. ⚠️ **A grep for the FIX vocabulary cannot find code that never adopted it. Sweep for the shape of the defect, not the shape of the remedy.**
- ⚠️ **THE ORIGINAL DIAGNOSIS WAS FALSE AND A HARD STOP CAUGHT IT BEFORE A 15-SURFACE CHANGE SHIPPED.** *(Superseded text, kept for history: "ROOT CAUSE UNVERIFIED, AND THE TWO CANDIDATES TAKE DIFFERENT FIXES.* Establish first: **(a)** a formatter exists and this surface bypasses it, or **(b)** no formatter exists and every surface renders raw. If (a), the fix is one call site plus a sweep for other bypasses; if (b), the fix is a shared formatter consumed everywhere. **Do not assume — the meal-builder quantity precedent (3f-2) is exactly this shape: a validity computation existed and the save path simply never consulted it.**
- ⚠️ **ARCHITECTURAL RULING ON THE RECORD (chat-Claude's call, §1 delegation): ROUND AT RENDER, NEVER ROUND-AND-STORE.** Storing a rounded macro makes every downstream consumer inherit one screen's precision decision. **Same reasoning as the scale-at-render servings invariant** — the stored value stays the honest number and each surface formats for its own context. ⚠️ **This also forecloses a "fix it on the server" reading of Hans's note:** the server may legitimately return the raw sum; the display is what owes a decimal.
- **Related:** the scale-at-render servings invariant · D-WS9-105 / BUG-059 (both also live at the ingredient-quantity/unit layer, but neither is this — those are *data* defects, this is a *presentation* defect).

### BUG-061 — Find Similar offers the user the very meal they are replacing ✅ **FIXED (`641aa3d`, device-confirmed August 6, 2026)**

- **Surface:** mobile `SwapMealSheet.tsx`, Similar mode (Different mode checked and clean).
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED.** Minted and fixed in the same block, WS9 3f-4b.
- **Symptom:** swapping *"Caprese Chicken with Garlic Roasted Potatoes…"* for a similar meal returned **a row with that same title** among the candidates. The user was offered the meal they were trying to get rid of.
- ✅ **ROOT CAUSE VERIFIED — two filters, two different keys.** Source exclusion filtered on **`id`** (`meal.id !== sourceMealId`); the title de-duplication added in 3f-4 filtered on **normalized title**. Hans's library contains **distinct records sharing a title** (24 duplicate-title clusters measured), so a duplicate record of the source passed the id filter, and the title de-dupe never saw it as the source. **Fix: `excludesSource()` now filters by id AND normalized title, using the same `normalizeMealTitleKey` as the de-dupe so the two agree.**
- ⚠️ **THE GENERALIZABLE FAILURE: two filters over the same collection keyed on different notions of identity.** Neither was wrong alone. **When adding a second filter to a list, check what key the existing filters use.**
- **Priority:** P2 — cosmetic-looking, but it undermines trust in the feature at the exact moment the user is asking it for help.
- **Cross-ref:** BUG-058 (the corpus problem this sits next to) · `lib/meals/dedupeByTitle.ts`.

### BUG-062 — Every other bottom-sheet `Modal` still stops above the Android navigation bar ✅ **FIXED — P2**

⚠️ **RESOLVED `d7a831c`, DEVICE-CONFIRMED BY HANS August 7, 2026** — *"gaps are gone and look good."* **11 of 14 Modals flagged** (the real inventory was fourteen, not the three this entry originally named — ⚠️ **do not treat a named list in canon as complete without a grep**). ⚠️ **`SortDropdown` DELIBERATELY SKIPPED** — an anchored popover positioned from a `measureInWindow` anchor; covering the nav bar changes the window origin and could offset the menu, and it has no bottom-edge gap to fix. **Do not "complete" the sweep by flagging it.** Two beyond the ask (`ErrorFallback`, `GroceryGeneratingOverlay`) were flagged on CC's judgment and accepted.

- **Surface:** `AddMealsSheet`, `AddMealToPlanSheet`, `PlanPreviewModal`, and ⚠️ **any other bottom-anchored transparent `Modal` — the three named are NOT confirmed to be the complete list; grep every `<Modal` before assuming.**
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED** — `d7a831c`, device-confirmed by Hans August 7, 2026 (*"gaps are gone and look good"*) · **Priority:** **P2**. Minted August 6, 2026 as the **verified blast radius of BUG-056**, not as a new observation. ⚠️ **Status field corrected September 5, 2026 — it read 🔴 OPEN while the heading and body both read FIXED (see the header's status-hygiene note).**
- **Symptom:** on Android gesture-navigation, the underlying screen shows through a strip below the sheet's bottom edge.
- ✅ **ROOT CAUSE ALREADY VERIFIED (via BUG-056):** Expo SDK 54 forces edge-to-edge; `app.json`'s android block is `{}`; these sheets do not set `navigationBarTranslucent`, so the transparent Modal never reaches the screen bottom. **Fix is the same one-line treatment device-confirmed on `SwapMealSheet`: `statusBarTranslucent` + `navigationBarTranslucent`.**
- ⚠️ **A transparent Modal that is NOT bottom-anchored may not want this treatment** — centre-anchored dialogs should be evaluated, not swept.
- **Priority:** **P2.** ⚠️ **Judged against Hans's standing bar — *"a friend thinks it's amazing"*** — the same visible wrongness that made BUG-056 worth fixing exists on several more surfaces, and it is a one-line fix each.

- **Cross-ref:** **BUG-056** (same defect, fixed, device-confirmed).

### BUG-063 — Ask-Kiwi's text input has no Android keyboard "done" key ✅ **FIXED — P2**

⚠️ **RESOLVED `d7a831c`, DEVICE-CONFIRMED BY HANS August 7, 2026** — *"keyboard - looks good."* ⚠️ **The earlier claim that Android cannot render a done key on a `multiline` input was FALSIFIED by config diff** — the Preferences Wizard notes field is `multiline` and shows the checkmark because it sets `returnKeyType="done"` + `blurOnSubmit` + `onSubmitEditing={Keyboard.dismiss}`. `AskKiwiView` had **none of the three**. Fix landed in the **shared** `AskKiwiView`, so the inline sheet creator, standalone `/ask-kiwi` and dish Mode-A all inherit it. ⚠️ **Accepted trade:** the return key dismisses instead of inserting a newline — fine for a meal description, **worth knowing if a multi-paragraph input is ever added to this component.**

- **Surface:** `AskKiwiView` — shared by the inline sheet creator (`AskKiwiCreator`), the standalone `/ask-kiwi` route, and the dish Mode-A screen. ⚠️ **All three inherit it.**
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED** — `d7a831c`, device-confirmed by Hans August 7, 2026 (*"keyboard - looks good"*) · **Priority:** **P2**. Regression introduced in WS9 3f-4, surfaced by Hans on device August 6, 2026. ⚠️ **Status field corrected September 5, 2026 — heading/body said FIXED, this field said OPEN.**
- **Symptom:** no checkmark key on the Android keyboard. The user must tap outside to dismiss — and **on the sheet, tapping outside hits the backdrop and closes the sheet**, so there is no safe dismissal at all.
- ⚠️ **THE STATED NEED IS CONFIRMATION, NOT DISMISSAL.** Hans, verbatim: *"I hit the checkbox and it hides my keyboard and I know the text was saved where I wrote it."* **A fix that only hides the keyboard does not satisfy this.**
- ⚠️ **A CONFIDENT DIAGNOSIS WAS FALSIFIED BY ONE SCREENSHOT, AND THIS IS THE ENTRY'S REAL VALUE.** 3f-4b concluded that *"Android will render a newline key rather than a done key regardless of `returnKeyType`"* for a `multiline` input, and solved it with an in-layout Done control. **The Preferences Wizard's notes field is ALSO multiline and DOES show the checkmark** — Hans supplied a screenshot of each. **So the platform permits it and the difference is `TextInput` configuration.** ⚠️ **BEFORE ASSERTING A PLATFORM LIMITATION, CHECK WHETHER THE APP ALREADY DOES THE THING SOMEWHERE ELSE.**
- ⚠️ **ORIGIN — A TEST-HARNESS LIMITATION DETERMINED SHIPPED USER-FACING BEHAVIOR.** 3f-4 dropped `KeyboardAwareScrollViewCompat` because `react-native-keyboard-controller` CJS-requires the real `react-native` and breaks the node harness. **That library stays out** — but the correct response was a layout solution, not accepting the loss.
- **Priority:** **P2.** **Target:** WS9 **3f-4c.**
- **Cross-ref:** BUG-064 (same screen family, different defect) · D-WS9-018.

### BUG-064 — Preferences Wizard notes field does not scroll into view on focus ✅ **FIXED — P3**

⚠️ **RESOLVED `d7a831c`, DEVICE-CONFIRMED BY HANS August 7, 2026.** ⚠️ **The prompt's premise was wrong** — the wizard was **not** missing a keyboard-aware wrapper; `wizard.tsx:237` already wraps the form in `KeyboardAwareScrollViewCompat`. **The real defect was `bottomOffset` defaulting to 0**, so a focused caret sat flush against the keyboard top. Fixed with `bottomOffset={Spacing[6]}`. ⚠️ **THE SAME DEFAULT AFFECTS EVERY CONSUMER** — `ask-kiwi`, `meal-builder`, `dish-builder`, `import-text`. **A shared default in the compat wrapper would fix all of them; CC correctly declined to make that call unprompted.** Still unlogged as its own item — **fold into Part 2.**

- **Surface:** Kitchen Wizard → Set preferences → *"Anything specific for this plan?"*.
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED** — `d7a831c`, device-confirmed by Hans August 7, 2026 · **Priority:** **P3**. Found by Hans on device, August 6, 2026, while testing BUG-063 — **an incidental find, not the thing he was looking for.** ⚠️ **Status field corrected September 5, 2026 — heading/body said FIXED, this field said OPEN.**
- **Symptom:** Hans, verbatim: *"when I go to enter comments in the text at the bottom the screen doesn't auto scroll up, so I have to click in to type, the keyboard expands over the text box, and then I can move the screen to see what I'm typing."*
- ⚠️ **`react-native-keyboard-controller` IS OFF-LIMITS for the fix** — its native dependency breaks the node test harness and is the direct cause of BUG-063. Use `KeyboardAvoidingView` from the stubbed `react-native`, a `ScrollView` ref with `measureLayout`, or equivalent.
- ⚠️ **CHECK FOR SIBLINGS.** Any screen with a text input near the bottom plausibly has this. **Enumerate; fix the wizard in 3f-4c and report the rest.**
- **Priority:** **P3** — a workaround exists (scroll manually) and no data is lost. **Target:** WS9 **3f-4c.**

### BUG-065 — Meal titles truncate mid-word across the app, making distinct meals indistinguishable ✅ **FIXED — P2**

⚠️ **RESOLVED `17d9062` AFTER FOUR ITERATIONS, DEVICE-CONFIRMED BY HANS August 8, 2026.** **The fix that shipped is NOT the fix this entry prescribes below.** Two-line titles (`d7a831c`) shipped and **still truncated** — catalog titles run to 101 chars and two lines buys ~50. **Three subsequent approaches were tried and abandoned:** a generation-side length cap (**D-WS9-125**), a dish-name sub-line (**D-WS9-127**), and a sides-only sub-line (**D-WS9-126**, superseded within a day). ⚠️ **What finally worked was the simplest thing, ruled by Hans: `numberOfLines={3}` on the primitive's `row` variant** — rows grow **only** when the text needs it, so the height cost lands on roughly the longest 10% rather than uniformly. ⚠️ **The durable lesson is in D-WS9-127: two assumptions about title data were proposed and built on WITHOUT a query, and both were false.**

- **Surface:** ⚠️ **APP-WIDE — the two below are CONFIRMED, not exhaustive.** Confirmed: **Recipes → Meals** list view; **Plan Review** meal cards. Hans: *"meal cards everywhere have the text running off. we need to update that across the board… there may be more."*
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED** — `17d9062` after four iterations, device-confirmed by Hans August 8, 2026 · **Priority:** **P2**. ⚠️ **The fix that shipped is NOT the fix this entry prescribes below** — read the body, not the prescription. Found by Hans on device August 6, 2026; **WS9 3f-4b fixed the swap sheet only**, the earlier scoped attempt. ⚠️ **Status field corrected September 5, 2026 — heading/body said FIXED, this field said OPEN.**
- **Symptom:** single-line titles with ellipsis truncation cut off the differentiating words. Three consecutive rows all read *"Air Fryer Crispy Chicken Tender…"* — identical on screen, different meals (35 min, 28 min, 28 min). **The user cannot tell them apart without opening each one.**
- ⚠️ **WHY IT IS WORSE THAN A COSMETIC ISSUE:** catalog titles are long and descriptive by construction (*"Brown Sugar and Smoked Paprika…"*, *"Summer Lentil Salad with Cherry T…"*), and **plan meals are saved to the user's library**, so a returning user's list fills with near-identical prefixes. **The truncation point is systematically where the information is.**
- **Fix:** two-line titles wherever meals render. ⚠️ **Rows will grow. That is accepted and ruled** — **do NOT compensate** by shrinking thumbnails, dropping macros, or tightening padding; those are the density decisions deferred until meal images land.
- ⚠️ **Where a fixed-height card genuinely cannot take two lines, report the surface and leave it — do not invent a different treatment per surface.**
- **Priority:** **P2.** **Target:** WS9 **3f-4c.**
- **Cross-ref:** the deferred density ruling (images-first) · BUG-035 (the other app-wide typography sweep).

### BUG-091 — Home's "this week" card body is not tappable; it should open the plan-instance meal detail

- **Date:** August 17, 2026 · **Surface:** `components/ActivePlanStrip.tsx` · **Found by:** Hans, on device.
- **Symptom:** `Start cooking` and `View plan` both work, but **tapping the card itself does nothing.** The card looks like an object you can open and isn't.
- ⚠️ **Same defect class as three others found this session** — a surface that renders as interactive and silently no-ops: composted meal rows, and (pre-2e) `Get Groceries Online`. **Silence is the shared failure; the user cannot distinguish "not tappable" from "broken."**
- **Root cause + fix, from Phase 0:** both branches return a plain `<View style={styles.card}>` (`:72`, `:143`). ✅ **Every id is already plumbed** — `HeroModel.today` carries `planId`, `planItemId`, `planName` and `meal`, and `heroState.ts:19-22` states `planItemId` is *"threaded so the today card opens Meal Detail with full plan-item context."* **Built for this tap and never wired.** Route shape exists at `PlanReviewMealRow.tsx:61-75`.
- ⚠️ **Two things to watch:** the Home push will lack `servingsOverride` (not on `MealListItemSchema`); Meal Detail resolves plan context server-side via `useMeal(mealId, planItemId)` so this is *likely* fine — **CC labelled it unverified and did not trace it.** And **`ActivePlanStrip.test.ts` exists** (contrary to chat-Claude's guard list) — two tests count and index pressables and **will go red correctly; update deliberately, do not weaken.** Nested-press behaviour cannot be proven by the harness (it stubs `Pressable` as a passthrough) — **device-test item.**
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED** · **Priority:** **P2** · **Cross-ref:** D-WS9-163 · D-WS9-167.
- **Resolution (Block 2e, two parts).** Part 2 (`c1adc99`) wired the tap on the **meal-today branch only**, scoping it to the meal block so the two buttons stayed siblings rather than nested children. ⚠️ **The no-meal-today branch was left unwired and a test asserted that absence on purpose** (*"it has no meal to open"*) — correct reasoning for a meal destination, and Hans re-found the gap on device: *"I only see the View Plan action and the card body doesn't tap."* Part 4 (`8bba1f0`) wired the second branch to **Plan Review** (`planId` was already plumbed; `planItemId` cannot exist there). The pinning test was **inverted, not deleted**, with the superseded reasoning recorded in place.
- ⚠️ **THE TRANSFERABLE LESSON: A TWO-BRANCH COMPONENT NEEDS THE FIX VERIFIED ON BOTH BRANCHES.** The audit passed, 1086 tests were green, and half the defect shipped anyway — because the branch that was fixed was the one anyone thought to check. **Device testing found it; nothing else could have.**

### BUG-092 — `size="sm"` did not make Compost smaller; `fullWidth` defaults true, so it became the LARGEST control on the screen

- **Date:** August 17, 2026 · **Surface:** `app/plan/[id].tsx` / `components/Button.tsx` · **Found by:** Hans, on device, from a screenshot.
- **Symptom:** D-WS9-157 ruled Compost *"visually smaller than the other four."* `size="sm"` correctly shrank padding and font — **but `fullWidth` still defaults true, so it stretches edge-to-edge while the four peers sit at half width.** Net effect: **the destructive action reads as the screen's primary.** Exact inversion of the ruling.
- ⚠️ **The lesson generalises: a size prop is not a size change when an orthogonal layout prop dominates it.** The equivalence test CC wrote (md-equals-no-prop) correctly proved `sm` didn't disturb existing consumers — **it could not detect that `sm` failed to achieve its purpose.**
- **Related, disclosed by CC, not yet ruled:** Compost also lost its terracotta destructive treatment (`Palette.button.destructive` exists in tokens but `Button`'s `Variant` union doesn't wire it). Safety net intact — active-plan confirm Alert + undo toast. **"Destructive action styled identically to its neighbours" deserves its own ruling.**
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED** · **Priority:** **P2** · **Cross-ref:** D-WS9-157 · D-WS9-162 · D-WS9-166.
- **Resolution (Block 2e Parts 3 + 4).** Part 3 (`86485b0`) removed Compost from the button cluster entirely and re-rendered it as a **quiet right-aligned text link** — the approved mockup's treatment, not a smaller button. Part 4 (`a461cc0`) moved that link **inside the action panel, bottom-right**. Behaviour byte-unchanged throughout: same handler, active-plan confirm, undo toast.
- ⚠️ **The disclosed sub-issue — Compost carrying no destructive treatment — was resolved by DEMOTION rather than by wiring `Palette.button.destructive`.** A destructive action that is quiet and confirmed is safer than one that is loud; the terracotta emphasis on that screen belongs to `Prep and Cook`. `Palette.button.destructive` remains unwired in `Button`'s `Variant` union and is now a known orphan.
- **Contrast note:** the link measured 5.90:1 on paper; after the Part 4 move onto the `sage[100]` panel it measures **5.06:1**, still past AA. The stale figure in the code comment was corrected in the same commit.

### BUG-099 — Tell Kiwi `parse_intent` 502: the schema required `reason` inside an optional object, and the model legitimately sent `{}`

- **Date:** August 18, 2026 · **Surface:** `artifacts/api-server/src/lib/ai/schemas/tellKiwi.ts` · **Found by:** Hans, from a pasted server log.
- **Symptom:** `POST /api/wizard/build-from-text` returned **502** — the user-facing *"Kiwi got distracted, try again?"* — after `wizard.directed.parse_intent` failed Zod validation on its retry. Hans hit it twice in one session.
- ⚠️ **THE LOG LIED ABOUT THE PATH, AND THAT IS THE LESSON.** The error read `fieldErrors: { needsClarification: ["Required"] }`, which looks like a missing top-level key. The outer key was **already `.optional()`**; the required field was the **nested `reason`**. `.flatten()` groups issues by `path[0]`, collapsing `["needsClarification","reason"]` to `needsClarification`. **A flattened Zod error is not a path — chat-Claude's first diagnosis from the log was wrong and CC's byte-identical reproduction settled it.**
- ⚠️ **NOT A ONE-OFF. 24 calls since June 12: 58% needed a retry, 12.5% exhausted and 502'd**, with failures in July as well as August. A one-in-eight failure rate on PRD §9's highest-priority path, live for two months.
- **Root cause:** the prompt says *"omit entirely"* for the three non-clarifying scenarios and demonstrates omission four times. The model emitted `{}` anyway — **nondeterministic output the schema had no spelling for.** Prompt rewording could not close it and none was made; **no reseed was required.**
- ⚠️ **THE MOBILE CLIENT DECIDED THE FIX.** It re-validates with its own Zod requiring `reason`, so tolerating `{}` and forwarding it would have **relocated the 502 to a client-side parse error.** Normalising to `undefined` was forced, not chosen — every one of the six downstream readers already handles absent.
- **Fix (`43341fb`):** a `z.preprocess` normaliser collapsing `{}` / `null` / any object without a usable `reason` → `undefined`. Genuine clarifications validate unchanged and pass `options` through intact. +10 tests, all proven by deliberate breaks.
- ⚠️ **ONE ACCEPTED DATA LOSS, DISCLOSED BY CC RATHER THAN BURIED:** an `overflow` payload carrying `options` but no `reason` now **silently drops the options** — the user gets their plan without swap chips instead of a 502. Judged strictly better than an error; CC declined to synthesize a `reason` string, which would have invented user-facing copy.
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED** · **Priority:** **P1** · **Cross-ref:** BUG-100.

---

### BUG-100 — the retry mechanism fed the model a flattened error, instructing it to do the opposite of what the prompt asked

- **Date:** August 18, 2026 · **Surface:** `artifacts/api-server/src/lib/ai/runAICall.ts` · **Found by:** CC, unprompted, while investigating BUG-099.
- **Symptom:** on a validation failure the shared helper built its retry instruction from `error.flatten()`. For BUG-099 that told the model, verbatim, that **`needsClarification` was `Required`** — the precise opposite of the prompt's *"omit entirely."*
- ⚠️ **THIS IS NOT A TUNING CHOICE. THE RETRY MECHANISM WAS SUPPLYING FALSE INFORMATION**, and it did so for **every AI call in the server** — 14 call sites across 10 production modules. It is a plausible mechanism for BUG-099's poor recovery rate, though **that link is inference, not measurement.**
- **Fix (`22a7eef`):** emit `error.issues` with **full dotted paths** (`needsClarification.reason: Required`), including array indices, capped at 20 issues with an *"and N more"* tail so a runaway response cannot bloat the retry prompt. +4 tests.
- ⚠️ **SCOPE WIDENED BEYOND THE RULING, DELIBERATELY AND DISCLOSED.** The same rendering flows to the exhaustion `WARN` and to `AICallFailure.internalError`. CC let it rather than maintain two renderings — **and the full path is exactly what would have made BUG-099 legible from Hans's original paste instead of requiring a reproduction.**
- **Verified:** no consumer parses or pattern-matches the feedback string; the 25 other `.flatten()` sites are HTTP-400 request-body validation on a different contract and were deliberately untouched.
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED** · **Priority:** **P2** · **Cross-ref:** BUG-099 · BUG-101.

### BUG-109 — a COMPOSTED plan stayed the "This Week" winner; `resolveThisWeekWinnerId` never filtered archived

- **Date:** August 19, 2026 · **Surface:** `api-server/src/lib/planDates.ts:171-186` · **Found by:** CC, Phase 0 **(mobile session, reading api-server code)**.
- ⚠️ **P1. Verified negative, quoted: `grep -c "isArchived" src/lib/planDates.ts` → `0`.** Compost sets `status:"past"`, `compostedAt`, `isArchived:true` and **leaves `startDate`/`endDate` untouched**, so a composted plan whose dates still covered today **remained the This-Week winner** — Home rendered it with `Start Cooking` / `Prep and Cook` / `Grocery List` all pointed at a dead plan.
- **Blast radius, 9 callers:** `home.ts:146` · `plans.ts:284` · `plans.ts:417` · `plans.ts:807` · `plans.ts:1149` · `groceryLists.ts:456,604` · `wizard.ts:1901` (**activation demotion — a composted plan could be "demoted" and could block a live one from winning**).
- ✅ **FIXED** in `2c3f1de`. ⚠️ **The ordering trap resolved in our favour:** `plans.ts:807` calls the resolver **before** its own update (`:815`), so the row under deletion is still live when resolved and `wasActive` stays correct — **no `includeArchived` option needed.** ⚠️ **A route test STAYED GREEN under the deliberate break because the delete stub's `update()` never applied the write to its fixture; CC fixed the fake and the break then turned 4 tests RED, including a pre-existing one.** Device-tested: **items 21 and 22 PASS.**
- ⚠️ **CANON WAS WRONG ABOUT THE CASCADE: compost ARCHIVES the grocery list** (`plans.ts:809-834`, `updateMany → status:"archived"`), it does not delete it, and `GET /grocery-lists/:id` has no archived filter.
- ⚠️ **CONSEQUENCE — IT REOPENED AND THEN CLOSED SOMETHING.** The two composted-screen entry points existed *because* of this leak; fixing it **made that screen unreachable again** (device item 23). Hans ruled the screen stays and is trusted as built — **D-WS9-173**.
- ⚠️ **AND IT CREATED TRAFFIC TO A HOLE: BUG-118.** Composting now drops the user into Home's empty-hero state.
- ⚠️ **THE LESSON: THE LIST GATE WAS RIGHT AND IRRELEVANT.** `my_plans` correctly filters `isArchived:false` (`planQueries.ts:320`), and that correct gate is *why* nobody looked further — **the winner resolver is a separate query the same route runs alongside it. A guard on one query says nothing about its neighbour.**
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED** (`2c3f1de`) · was **P1** · **Cross-ref:** D-WS9-173 · D-WS9-159 · BUG-118.

### BUG-112 — fire-and-forget plan mutators swallowed rejections

- **Date:** August 19, 2026 · **Surface:** `app/plan/[id].tsx:1512-1526` + 3 more · **Found by:** CC, Phase 0 (mobile).
- **Symptom:** `handleAssignDay`, `handleCompostFromPlan`, `handleSavePlanName` and `handleSaveDateRange` discarded rejections — **on a failed PATCH the optimistic state stood, nothing invalidated, and the user saw an edit persist that had not.**
- ⚠️ **THE HARDENING FOR THIS SHAPE ALREADY EXISTED AND HAD BEEN APPLIED TO ONE HANDLER, NOT THE CLASS** (`applyMealReplacement`, `plan/[id].tsx:1440-1452`, WS9 3d Part 4) — **the same instance-not-class miss that shipped half of BUG-091.**
- ✅ **FIXED, folded into `2aae661`** per **replace-don't-layer**: Task 1 moved these onto the runner, which already rolls back and rethrows, so each needed exactly **one** thing — a `.catch` that toasts. **No second error path beside a rollback that already handles it.**
- ⚠️ **IT CAUGHT A REAL HOLE ON THE WAY:** `handleCookThisWeek`'s optimistic flip **used to survive a failure**, so the plan read as active when it was not. `applyMealReplacement` also moved onto the runner and its `planQuery.refetch()` was deleted.
- **Whole-client `void <mutator>(...)` sweep: one instance remains and it is the worst — `app/meal/[id].tsx:447`, logged as BUG-120.** `meals.tsx:188`, `meal/[id].tsx:497`, `grocery-list/[id].tsx:553` all have `.catch`; `plan/[id].tsx:1423` is safe and documented.
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED** (`2aae661`) · **Cross-ref:** BUG-120 · BUG-119 · BUG-091.

### BUG-113 — plan mutations that changed rendered data did not bump `revisionId`

- **Date:** August 19, 2026 · **Surface:** `api-server/src/routes/plans.ts` recalc-macros · **Found by:** CC, Phase 0 (api-server).
- ⚠️ **THE PREMISE OF THIS ENTRY WAS TWO-THIRDS WRONG, AND IT CAME FROM A LINE-CITED PHASE 0 CLAIM.** The Phase 1 sweep found `PATCH /plans/:id` **already bumps** (`plans.ts:1136`, minus a deliberate name-only carve-out) and `DELETE /plans/:id` **already bumps** (`:816`, inside the compost tx). **Only `POST /plans/:id/recalc-macros` was real** — and it returned no `revisionId` at all, while persisting `Dish.*PerServing` and re-summing `Meal.*PerServing`, both of which sit in `MEAL_LIST_SELECT`. **So plan and home payloads changed while the version stood still**, and `lastGeneratedFromPlanRevisionId` (grocery drift) shared the blind spot.
- ✅ **FIXED** in `83fb435`. ⚠️ **CC gated the bump on `persistedMealIds.size > 0` rather than unconditional — a decision not in the prompt, disclosed, accepted.** `revisionId` is also the prep-week structure's cache key (`cooking.ts:221`), so an unconditional bump would **discard an AI-backed prep-week cache on every all-cached recalc that changed nothing.**
- **Reported, not fixed, both needing rulings:** `PUT/DELETE /plans/:planId/prep-week/completions` carries **no version signal** though completions drive `derivedPrepStatus`; and **`PATCH /me/dishes/:id` has the same shape with no `bumpPlanId` escape hatch** — it edits a canonical Dish reachable from plans and would need a "which plans contain this dish" fan-out.
- ⚠️ **AN ECHOED-BUT-UNBUMPED VERSION IS WORSE THAN NO VERSION FIELD**, because it invites exactly the caching a correct field would license.
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED** (`83fb435`) · **Cross-ref:** BUG-104.

### BUG-114 — `assignedDate` was written by no server code path, and read FIRST by `GET /home`

- **Date:** August 19, 2026 · **Surface:** `api-server/src/routes/home.ts:97`, `plans.ts:144` · **Found by:** CC, Phase 0 (api-server).
- **Symptom:** two-shaped search found **27 occurrences in `src/` — every one a `select`, projection, type or read.** The only `data:` appearance merely **copies `current.assignedDate`** during a `mealId` swap; `PatchPlanItemBody` does not accept it. **Nothing wrote it.** Yet `resolveTodaysItem` matched on it **first** and `computeMacroDailyAverage` keyed on `assignedDate ?? assignedDayOfWeek`.
- ✅ **FIXED** in `a9ae14c`. Verified before removing: **9 of 371 (2.4259%), all seed rows, all agreeing on UTC weekday, zero disagreements.** Reads removed from both; **column and projections left in place** (dropping it is a migration and a separate ruling).
- ⚠️ **IT TOOK TWO REAL BUGS WITH IT THAT NOBODY WAS LOOKING FOR.** (1) `resolveTodaysItem` truncated the UTC-midnight value with `startOfDay()`, **which truncates in LOCAL time — so on any server west of UTC the date branch already matched the wrong day.** (2) `computeMacroDailyAverage` mixed two key spaces in one `Set`: an ISO string and `"Monday"` counted as **two assigned days for the same Monday**, inflating the divisor and understating the average. **Both were live behaviour, not theory.**
- **Reported, not fixed:** `planningContext.ts:306-313` is a **third reader**, outside the named scope, with the same exposure.
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED** (`a9ae14c`).
