### BUG-156 — The wizard writes no meal description, so the subtext feature is invisible on the very screen it was requested for

- **Date:** August 25, 2026 · **Surface:** meal generation (wizard path), server-side · **Found by:** CC, while scoping BUG-153 — ⚠️ **it measured the data before building the render, and the measurement changed the shape of the work.**
- **The measurement, by `sourceType`, description EMPTY:** ⚠️ **`wizard` 173/185 = 94%** · `batch_generated` 1/99 = 1% · ⚠️ **`curated` 8/8 = 100%** · `manual` 0/7 = 0%.
- ⚠️ **WHY THIS MATTERS MORE THAN A NORMAL DATA GAP: THE PLAN-REVIEW SCREEN HANS ASKED FOR IS THE "USE MY PREFERENCES" PATH, WHICH IS WIZARD-GENERATED.** So BUG-153's render, shipped alone, **displays nothing for 94% of the meals on the exact screen that prompted the request.** The render is **necessary but not sufficient**; there is a server-side writer gap underneath it.
- ⚠️ **AND PER D-WS9-186 (FORWARD FIXES ONLY) THE 173 EXISTING WIZARD MEALS WILL NEVER GAIN DESCRIPTIONS.** Fixing the writer makes **new** plans carry subtext. **Since fresh plans are now the agreed test substrate, that is fine — but it means this cannot be device-verified on an existing plan, only on a newly generated one.**
- **`curated` at 8/8 empty is a separate, smaller gap** — hand-authored content rather than a writer defect. Folded here rather than minted separately; 8 rows.
- ⚠️ **A THIRD FACE OF THIS GAP, FOUND DURING BUG-153's BUILD: the WIZARD DRAFT PAYLOAD has no `description` field at all**, so `wizardDraftReviewAdapter` leaves it undefined by necessity. **The gap is therefore at least two layers deep — the draft payload shape AND the generator's output — not just an unpopulated column.** Scope the fix to both.
- ⚠️ **HANS RE-FRAMED THIS ON August 26, 2026, AND HIS FRAMING IS PROBABLY THE RIGHT ONE — IT CHANGES THE FIX FROM "ADD A FIELD" TO "WHY ARE THERE TWO PATHS."** Verbatim: *"the wizard generates meals, and it should be doing the same meal generation that the catalog run did. it should be creating description text every time and putting that on the meals it creates. and those meals go in the catalog and can be used again by other users. That's how it should work, but something may not be wired yet."*
- ⚠️ **THE MEASUREMENT SUPPORTS HIM AND IS THE STRONGEST EVIDENCE HERE: `batch_generated` is 1/99 EMPTY (99% have descriptions) while `wizard` is 173/185 EMPTY (94% do not). The same product, generating the same artefact, with a ~95-point gap. That is not a missing column — that is two different generation paths.**
- **Two factual questions to settle BEFORE designing anything, both cheap:** (1) **do the wizard and the catalog run actually use different generation code/prompts, or the same one called differently?** (2) **do wizard-generated meals enter the shared catalog and become reusable by other users, as Hans expects?** ⚠️ **If (2) is false, that is a larger finding than the description gap itself.**
- ⚠️ **PROBE 1 (August 26) ANSWERED (1) AND LEFT (2) OPEN — DO NOT OVER-READ ITS HEADLINE.** (1) ✅ **VERIFIED: two fully divergent paths.** Catalog run: `storeFill.ts:728` → `store.generate_meal`. Wizard: `wizardExpansion.ts:466` → `wizard.candidate.expand`. No shared generation function, no shared prompt. (2) Probe 1 found 0 of 197 `source='wizard'` meals public or catalog-owned — **but that is the expected result even under a WORKING copy-on-publish design**, because a published copy would be a new catalog-owned row under a different source value. **114 of 1,546 meals were never classified (≈64 of them public beyond the batch population), and canon names an optional `publishMealToStore` write-back reachable from `materializeWizardDraft` (latency program §2.2; `mealFork.ts:209` propagates lineage on `live_writeback` rows).** Whether that path fires, and what gates it, is Probe Block 2 (D). The 18 lineage-less wizard rows in BUG-162's duplicate groups are evidence it rarely fires — a rate question, not zero.
- ⚠️ **THE 173/185 FIGURE IS AN ALL-TIME AGGREGATE SPANNING D-WS9-124's AUGUST 8 PROMPT FIX — THE §27.5 / BUG-101 SHAPE.** Description instructions were already added to `wizard.candidate.expand` and shipped August 8; the post-go-live rate is unmeasured (and goes live at reseed time, not commit time, per §7). Probe Block 2 (E) dates the gap by `createdAt` against the actual reseed date and, if it persists post-fix, names the layer that drops it (the draft-payload gap above is one candidate).
- ⚠️ **HANS CONSIDERED AND REJECTED FILLER TEXT, TALKING HIMSELF OUT OF IT MID-SENTENCE** (*"maybe some standard text 'This meal was created just for you.' but honestly.. thinking about it now"*). **No placeholder. Fix the generator.**
- **Likely shape of the fix:** the wire schema already carries `description`; the wizard's generation simply does not populate it. ⚠️ **That points at §7 territory — `aiPrompts.ts`, a reseed and a diff-driven version bump — not a code-only change. Treat the prompt-cache boundary as load-bearing and measure prefix tokens with real `count_tokens`, never an estimate.**
✅ **RESOLVED (Probe 2, August 27) — BOTH FRAMING QUESTIONS ANSWERED, AND BOTH ANSWERS OVERTURNED THE ALARM.**

**(1) The description gap was ALREADY FIXED on August 8 — three weeks before it was investigated.** `wizard.candidate.expand` version history: v1 (06-12) · v2 (07-09) · v3 (07-25) all `mentionsDescription=false`; **v4 (2026-08-08T11:32:21Z) active, `mentionsDescription=TRUE`.** `LLMCallLog.promptVersion` corroborates independently (v3 → v4 on 08-08), so seed and go-live coincide. Post-go-live: **0 of 15** wizard meals empty (08-10 · 08-17 · 08-19 · 08-21 · 08-23 · 08-24, every day 0%), against 100% empty on every day from 06-26 to 08-02. A clean step function.
- ⚠️ **THE 173/185 FIGURE — AND ITS RESTATEMENT AS 182/197 — WAS AN ALL-TIME AGGREGATE SPANNING ITS OWN FIX. IT IS RETIRED; DO NOT CITE IT AGAIN.** The server chain is complete end to end: prompt v4 ✅ → expand output schema (`wizard.ts:250, :369`, `z.string().max(200).optional()`) ✅ → materialize write (`wizardActivation.ts:221`, `description: m.description ?? null`) ✅ → `Meal` row ✅.
- ⚠️ **THE PROCESS LESSON IS THE EXPENSIVE PART: D-WS9-124 RECORDED THIS FIX CORRECTLY AND WAS RIGHT ALL ALONG.** Chat-Claude and CC each re-derived a stale rate on top of accurate canon, and the false alarm survived a full probe block. §27.5's rule — break every rate out by version, build or date **before** acting on it — is what would have caught it on day one.

**(2) Hans's catalog expectation IS met; the A4 headline was WRONG.** `publishMealToStore` is defined at `mealFork.ts:133` and has **one production call site**, `wizardActivation.ts:382`, inside `materializeWizardDraft`'s per-slot loop. The gate is `if (slot.writeBack)`, where `writeBack: !sid` (`wizardFinalize.ts:149`) — **true iff the slot was live-generated**; a store-filled slot forks instead and correctly does not re-publish. It fires: **51 pool copies across 15 distinct days**, 07-16 → 08-24, at 1:1 with live-generated slots over the recent window.
- ⚠️ **WHY THE PROBE-1 HEADLINE WAS WRONG, AND IT IS A REUSABLE SHAPE: it filtered on `sourceType='wizard'`, and a published copy is BY CONSTRUCTION A DIFFERENT ROW WITH A DIFFERENT SOURCE VALUE (`live_writeback`).** Wizard meals are never themselves published — **a copy is** — so "zero public wizard-source rows" was the expected result of a working design. **A filter that excludes the evidence produces a confident negative.**
- ⚠️ **THE ONE REAL CONSEQUENCE, AND IT LANDS ON BUG-162: the published copy carries NULL `sourceStoreMealId` AND NULL `dishFamilyKey`, asserted by design at `mealFork.test.ts:464`.** A user's live-generated meal and its own pool copy are unlinked **in both directions**.
- **Secondary, lower priority:** because write-backs carry no `dishFamilyKey`, those 51 pool meals fall back to title-only matching in the repeat-avoidance walk (D-WS9-073). A degradation, not a break — **written down here so nobody later measures rotation quality and blames the prompt.**

**What survives:** the client half only — **BUG-163**.

- **Status:** ✅ **RESOLVED — server half was already fixed (v4, August 8); catalog publishing verified working; client half split to BUG-163** · **Priority:** **P2** · **Cross-ref:** BUG-153 · BUG-162 · **BUG-163** · D-WS9-073 · D-WS9-124 · D-WS9-186.

### BUG-163 — The wizard draft-review payload drops `description` client-side

- **Date:** August 27, 2026 · **Surface:** wizard draft review (pre-save), mobile · **Found by:** CC, Probe Block 2 E4 — **the last surviving face of BUG-156, after its server half proved already fixed.**
- **Symptom:** a saved meal has a description; the **pre-save draft-review screen cannot show one**, because the client wire never carries the field. So the subtext appears only *after* the user commits — on the screen where the decision is already made.
- ✅ **VERIFIED, TWO SHAPES, AND FROM THE OPPOSITE DIRECTION TOO.** `rg --no-ignore --hidden -n "description" lib/api/wizard.ts lib/plans/wizardDraftReviewAdapter.ts` → **exit 1, zero matches**; `find lib -type f … | xargs grep -c description` → `lib/api/wizard.ts:0`, `lib/plans/wizardDraftReviewAdapter.ts:0`. **The opposite direction is what makes it conclusive:** the same schema carries `title`, `cuisineType`, `estimatedTimeMinutes` and `servings` — so the field is absent, not merely unmatched by a mis-shaped query.
- ⚠️ **THE SERVER CHAIN IS COMPLETE — THIS IS PURELY CLIENT-SIDE.** Prompt v4 → expand schema (`wizard.ts:250, :369`) → materialize write (`wizardActivation.ts:221`) → `Meal` row all carry it (BUG-156). `wizardDraftReviewAdapter` leaves `description` undefined **by necessity**, because its input shape has no such field.
- ⚠️ **THIS IS THE THIRD FACE OF ONE GAP AND THE ONLY ONE LEFT.** The other two — the generator not authoring a description, and the `MealListItem` render — are both shipped and closed. **Scope this to the wire and the adapter; do not reopen either.**
- **Relationship to BUG-159:** distinct surfaces, same user-facing complaint (*"I have to save and wait before I know if I'll like it"*). BUG-159 is the plan-**chooser**; this is the draft **review**. Worth building in one pass.
- ✅ **FIXED `dbd389e` (unpushed).** `description: z.string().optional()` on the mobile `WizardExpandEnrichedMealSchema` (mirroring the server's `.max(200).optional()`) and `description: meal.description ?? undefined` in the adapter — **the exact shape `reviewPlanAdapter` already uses for a saved plan** (§27.2 reuse). Pinned by break 7, which went red. ⚠️ **Expect it to render TRUNCATED TO ONE LINE — that is BUG-158, not a failure of this fix.**
⚠️ **FAILED THE DEVICE PASS (Hans, August 27) — REOPENED.** Path walked: **Home → "Use my preferences" → review and save → Draft Review.** **No description text renders at all.**
- ⚠️ **CHAT-CLAUDE'S DIAGNOSIS, UNVERIFIED: CC VERIFIED THE WRONG CHAIN.** Its *"server chain confirmed complete"* traced **expand → materialize → `Meal` row** — the **SAVED** path. **Draft Review renders BEFORE save, from the draft payload, which is a different object.** ⚠️ **BUG-156's own entry recorded exactly this: *"the WIZARD DRAFT PAYLOAD has no `description` field at all"* — and flagged the gap as "at least two layers deep."** So the client now has a field to read and the server still is not sending it on that path.
- ⚠️ **THE SHAPE TO CARRY FORWARD: a wire fix was verified against the chain it was ASSUMED to use, not the chain the failing screen actually reads.** The mobile schema + adapter change is correct and stays; it is simply upstream of a payload that carries nothing. **Verify the DRAFT payload's shape before rebuilding anything client-side.**
✅ **FIXED `641f27f` (local) — August 27, 2026. ⚠️ AND CHAT-CLAUDE'S "WRONG CHAIN" DIAGNOSIS WAS HALF WRONG: THE CLIENT CHANGE WAS CORRECTLY PLACED ALL ALONG.**
- **The real layer:** **`composeStoreMealDetails` (`src/lib/store/storeMealDetails.ts`)** — neither the Prisma `select` nor the returned object mentioned `description`, while the live path spreads `{...liveMeal}` and kept it. **Fix: add it to both.**
- ✅ **PROVEN BY NATURAL EXPERIMENT, NOT BY INSPECTION:** draft `9c39547c` — **4 store-composed slots with NO description, 1 live slot WITH one.** Store-composition is the only discriminator. Hans's device draft `6c18df5d` was 4/4 store-composed, 0 descriptions.
- **The chain the screen actually reads** (traced, not assumed): `wizard-results.tsx:533` → `buildOpenDraftParams` → `JSON.stringify(expand.expanded)` **as a route param** → `parseDraftExpanded` → last block's adapter. ⚠️ **Draft mode NEVER fetches** (`usePlan("")` disabled), so the payload arrives entirely through the route param.
- **Source data was never the problem:** 1,140 of 1,188 pool meals (96%) carry a description; length min 72 / median 149 / **max 200, zero over** — `z.string().max(200)` holds.
- ⚠️ **TWO TESTS, AND THE REASON GENERALISES: the stub Prisma IGNORES `select`, so a test asserting only on composed output stays GREEN when the `select` half is broken.** Break B2 failed *only* the query-shape test (20 pass / 1 fail). **Any field-drop bug of this class needs a query-shape assertion, not just an output assertion.**
- ⚠️ **D-WS9-034 IS STALE — CORRECT IT:** the draft payload lives in `MealPlanInstance.wizardDraftPayload`, **not** overloaded onto `optimizationNotes` (null on all 6 drafts inspected).
- ⚠️ **NOT DEVICE-CONFIRMED** — proven by unit test and payload archaeology; no fresh draft was generated and looked at.
✅ **DEVICE-VERIFIED August 28, 2026 — descriptions render on Draft Review.** ⚠️ **ONE MEAL OF ~5 SHOWED NONE** (*"Beef and black bean enchiladas with red sauce and melted cheddar"*). ⚠️ **MOST LIKELY A DATA GAP, NOT A CODE GAP — and it must be checked before anyone reopens this: 48 of 1,188 pool meals (4%) carry NO description**, so one blank in five is consistent with the source row simply not having one. **One query settles it: does that meal's pool row have a `description`?** Do NOT assume either way.
- ⚠️ **HANS'S VERDICT ON THE FEATURE ITSELF: IT IS ON THE WRONG SCREEN.** Asked whether it helps him decide — *"not really, in all honesty. I think it's good to have there, but it would be much better if it was on the Plan Options screen meal cards. I think they would need to be bigger."* **The fix works; the placement was the weaker half of the ask.** ⚠️ **BUG-159 — the plan-CHOOSER — is now USER-VERIFIED as the higher-value placement, which raises it above this entry.**
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED and device-verified — but see BUG-159; the decision screen is where this belongs** · **Priority:** **P2** · **Cross-ref:** BUG-153 · BUG-156 · BUG-158 · **BUG-159** · BUG-173 · D-WS9-034 · **D-WS9-190**.

### BUG-165 — The AI merge discards the dropped sibling's provenance, leaving a row whose sources cannot account for its own quantity

- **Date:** August 27, 2026 · **Surface:** `groceryListAI.ts:445-450` · **Found by:** CC, Phase 0, **as the second-order damage behind BUG-142.**
- **Symptom:** when the AI merges two rows, the un-popped sibling is dropped **and its `GroceryListItemSource` rows go with it**. The surviving row's persisted quantity then covers contributions from sources it no longer lists — a 9-source need persisting 8 sources.
- ⚠️ **THIS IS WHAT MADE BUG-142 INVISIBLE FOR TWO BLOCKS.** The override-aware reconstruction summed the row's own declared provenance and got 7.75 against a stored 10.75 — **it was reading a record from which the evidence had already been deleted.** A verification harness that trusts persisted provenance cannot see this class at all.
- ⚠️ **AND IT IS A LIVE HAZARD FOR RECONCILE, NOT JUST FOR AUDITING.** `groceryReconcile.ts` decides `unchanged` from source signatures (BUG-126's territory). A row whose sources under-describe its quantity is exactly the input that logic is not built for.
- ⚠️ **`wasAiInferred` IS NOT A RELIABLE MARKER HERE** — CC flagged this against its own diagnostic: the flag is model-authored and passed through at `groceryListAI.ts:517-521`, so it indicates **AI-subset membership**, not which row the model actually touched.
- ✅ **FIXED `dbd389e` (unpushed) — STRUCTURALLY, NOT BY DETECTION, WHICH WAS THE ASK.** `GenerateListOutputItem` now carries **`sourceKeys`**, helper-attached after the AI pass on the same contract as the existing purchase-pack fields (documented precedent: *"the AI does not emit these"*, so a model emitting it cannot influence provenance). **A merged row declares every bucket it absorbed; both persist joins union across them.** ⚠️ **There is no third path: a sibling is either absorbed with its key carried, or its group was refused and it survives as its own row with its own key.**
- **§27.2 reuse:** `bucketKeyOf` already existed but was module-private and its formula had been **hand-inlined at four call sites**. CC exported it and replaced all four rather than adding a fifth copy — so the join key now has **one definition**.
- **Pinned by break 5** (disable the `sourceKeys` union) → red: `- 'kosher salt|tablespoon' missing from provenance`.
- ⚠️ **NOT YET PROVEN ON A LIVE REGENERATION.** The persist-join union is proven by unit test and by reading both call sites; the persisted-row harness still shows the 6 old disagreements and **will only clear on the next generation**. ⚠️ **And `groceryReconcile.ts`'s union path has NO dedicated test** — same shape as the route's, existing suite passes, but uncovered. **Worth a test before this is called closed.**
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED `dbd389e` — device pass pending; reconcile-path union untested** · **Priority:** **P2** · **Cross-ref:** BUG-126 · BUG-142 · BUG-164.

### BUG-168 — The staple-variant merge family folds whole peppercorns in with ground pepper

- **Date:** August 27, 2026 · **Surface:** `STAPLE_VARIANT_TO_BASE` / `groceryMerge.ts` · **Found by:** chat-Claude, auditing the build report's blast-radius enumeration.
- **Symptom (latent):** BUG-142's fix lets a staple variant inherit its base's conversion data and merge. The enumerated families are olive oil, salt, and — ⚠️ — **`freshly ground black pepper` · `ground black pepper` · `black peppercorns` → `black pepper`.**
- ⚠️ **WHOLE PEPPERCORNS AND GROUND PEPPER ARE NOT THE SAME PURCHASE.** A brine wanting peppercorns and a rub wanting ground pepper are two different things to buy; merging them tells the shopper to buy one. **The salt family raises the same question more mildly** (kosher vs flaky sea salt now merge, and the surviving row takes **whichever variant's display name came first**) — CC flagged the salt case honestly and did not extend it to pepper.
- ⚠️ **UNVERIFIED, AND ONE QUERY SETTLES IT: does base `black pepper` carry a code-table conversion?** If it does not, the fallback finds nothing and the pair still cannot merge — latent and harmless. **If it does, the merge is live.** Neither fires on the current 7 plans, so this is not a device-visible defect today.
- ⚠️ **SECONDARY, AND WORTH FIXING WHEREVER IT LANDS: the blast-radius table's `(convRef set)` / `(convRef NULL)` annotations are ambiguous about whether they describe the VARIANTS or the BASE.** Read one way they contradict Phase 0's finding that base salt carries `gramsPerCup: 273`. **Pin it before anyone reads that table as canon.**
- **The product question is Hans's, not a technical call** — device-test item 8 puts it to him.
- ✅ **CLOSED `77dab10` (local) — August 27, 2026, and CONFIRMED BY TEST RATHER THAN ASSUMED.** `black peppercorns` was dropped from the merge-grouping key, so peppercorns and ground pepper stay two rows; a test asserts it. ⚠️ **The other six pepper entries — `cracked` / `ground` / `freshly ground` / `fresh ground` / `cracked pepper` / `ground pepper` — were KEPT, because they are genuine synonyms for one container of ground black pepper.** ⚠️ **Chat-Claude's instruction to "remove the pepper family" would have discarded a correct fold; CC's classification caught it.**
- **Status:** ✅ **CLOSED via BUG-172's narrowing** · **Priority:** **P2** · **Cross-ref:** BUG-142 · BUG-167 · **BUG-172**.

### BUG-169 — The grocery list tells the user to buy water

- **Date:** August 27, 2026 · **Surface:** grocery generation · **Found by:** chat-Claude, reading Hans's device screenshots — **not reported by Hans, and he ruled it on sight.**
- **Symptom:** `1 bottle (16.9 oz) water · Pantry Staple · (4 quart)`, sourced from a pasta dish. ⚠️ **Two defects in one row:** water should not be a grocery line at all, **and** the pack math is a ~7× under-order (a 16.9 oz bottle against a 4-quart need).
- ⚠️ **HANS-RULED, VERBATIM:** *"we shouldn't tell someone to order water. I can't think of a need to order water for groceries for a meal."* **No exceptions discussed.**
- ⚠️ **HANS BELIEVES THIS IS A REGRESSION** — *"I think we tried to eliminate water ordering a long time ago, but apparently it's back."* ⚠️ **TREAT THAT AS A CLAIM TO VERIFY, NOT A FACT (§27).** An exclusion that existed and was lost is a different fix from one that never existed, and it changes whether anything else was lost with it. **§27.3 applies: one grep is not enough to conclude either way.**
- **The pack-math half is BUG-147's class** (a volume need against a container pack under-orders) and is moot here if water is excluded outright — **but the same shape will exist for stock, broth and milk, which ARE legitimately ordered.**
✅ **FIXED `4b598c7` (local) — August 27, 2026.** ✅ **AND THE REGRESSION CLAIM IS SETTLED: NOTHING WAS LOST.** Exactly one commit ever touched water in the grocery libs — **`2bb0306`, June 3, 2026**, which *added* it to `UNIVERSAL_STAPLES` so *"a recipe's '1/2 cup water' no longer renders as a buyable bottle."* ⚠️ **Right intent, wrong mechanism — a staple is a DIMMED row, not an ABSENT one** — and BUG-125's unconditional order line later made it shout. Two ignore-blind searches (33 and 10 raw hits, every one classified) plus `git log -S` on five candidate names found **no exclusion to restore.** Hans's instinct was half right and verifying it was worth the call.
- **The fix:** `NEVER_ORDER_CANONICALS` + `isNeverOrdered()` in `groceryStaples.ts`, with the skip at **bucket-build** in `groceryList.ts` so the row never exists at any downstream layer. ⚠️ **Water was REMOVED from `UNIVERSAL_STAPLES` rather than left beside the new rule (§10 replace-don't-layer) — and that turned out to be FORCED, not stylistic: the existing "flags every universal staple" test derives its expected count from the array, so layering would have broken it.**
- **Exact-string, never substring — 8 excluded:** `water` · `warm water` · `cold water` · `ice water` · `ice-cold water` · `boiling water` · `pasta cooking water` · `reserved pasta cooking water`. **Deliberately kept purchasable:** `rose water` · `kewra water` · ⚠️ **`cold sparkling water`** (CC's judgment call, flagged not buried — you buy a bottle). **Kept, not a match:** `watercress` · `seedless watermelon` · 4 × canned-tuna-in-water rows. **17 of 17 reconciled.**
- **Off-prompt and correct: the rule applies to PLAN-DERIVED ingredients only.** ⚠️ **A user who puts water in their recurring groceries has stated intent to buy it, and silently dropping that would be a worse bug.** Pinned by a test.
- **Measured `2026-08-27T21:19:40Z`:** 8 lists (was 7 — the population moved), **560 → 552 rows, exactly 8 removed lines, one water row per list and nothing else**, including `4eb910fa|water|4|quart`, the row from the device pass.
- **A pre-existing test was INVERTED** (WS7-5d Block 5's *"flags 'water' as a universal staple"*). **It was correct for the ruling in force in June; it changed because the ruling changed.**
- ⚠️ **KNOWN LIMITATION, ON THE RECORD: an authored exact-string list GOES STALE.** `lukewarm water` or `filtered water` from a future recipe would render fully buyable **with no signal**. No pattern-matcher was built — deliberately, since a substring rule would drop `rose water`, `watercress` and the tuna rows. **The durable never-order class belongs to D-WS9-189.**
- ⚠️ **`warm water` is PREVENTIVE and UNEXERCISED** — no current plan uses it, so it appears in no before/after diff. **Only plain `water` was proven live.**
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED `4b598c7` (local) — device pass pending** · **Priority:** **P2** · **Cross-ref:** BUG-125 · BUG-147 · **BUG-171** · D-WS9-189.

### BUG-171 — Every pantry staple renders a full order line, silently reversing the June intent

- **Date:** August 27, 2026 · **Surface:** `app/grocery-list/[id].tsx:1152` → `composePackName` · **Found by:** CC, small-block Phase 0, **while verifying Hans's water-regression claim — it is the finding next to the one being looked for.**
- **Symptom:** every `isUniversalStaple` row carries a pack line — `1 container (26 oz) Kosher salt`, `1 bag (4 lb) All-purpose flour`, `1 bottle (17 oz) Olive oil`, `1 bag (4 lb) sugar`, and the one that surfaced it, `1 bottle (16.9 oz) water` against a **4-quart** need.
- ⚠️ **THIS IS DRIFT, NOT A DESIGN.** `2bb0306` (June 3, 2026) added water to `UNIVERSAL_STAPLES` explicitly so *"a recipe's '1/2 cup water' no longer renders as a buyable bottle (same flag+dim path as salt/pepper)."* **`083d935` (BUG-125, August 23) made `composePackName` unconditional with no staple branch** — and reversed that intent app-wide. **Nobody connected the two changes.** ⚠️ **The row was always there; BUG-125 is what made it shout, which is exactly why it reads as a regression to the eye.**
- ✅ **RULED (Hans, August 27, 2026) — OPTION A: a staple shows the NEED, not a pack.** Target: `Kosher salt · Pantry Staple · (11 teaspoon)` with no `1 container (26 oz)`.
- ⚠️ **HIS RATIONALE GENERALISES FURTHER THAN THE FIX AND IS THE PART WORTH KEEPING, VERBATIM:** *"users might buy in bulk or only a little at a time, so it's a good call for pantry staples."* **Pack size for a staple is a PURCHASING decision that belongs to the user — not an assumption the app should make.** The list's job for a staple is to state what the week needs.
- **Scope:** affects salt, pepper, flour, sugar, olive oil, water and every other `UNIVERSAL_STAPLES` member. ⚠️ **Deliberately held OUT of the small block** — the water exclusion must not depend on it, and this is its own change.
- ⚠️ **DOES NOT CLOSE BUG-147.** Stock, broth and milk are **not** staples, still render packs, and still under-order a volume need against a container pack. **The under-order class survives this fix; it merely stops being visible on staples.**
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED — shipped August 27, 2026 in `988e724`** (alongside BUG-157 · BUG-106 · BUG-158). ⚠️ **THIS FIELD READ 🔴 OPEN — ruled, unbuilt FOR TEN DAYS AFTER THE FIX SHIPPED, and both the position block and a CC prompt inherited the error** — chat-Claude commissioned a rebuild of already-shipped work on September 6, and CC refused it. **Rebuilding would have created a second mechanism for one concern.** · **Priority:** **P2** · **Cross-ref:** BUG-125 · BUG-147 · BUG-169 · **BUG-209** · **D-WS9-221**.
- ✅ **VERIFIED SEPTEMBER 6, 2026 BY DELIBERATE BREAK, NOT BY READING.** `composePackName` carries `if (isPantryStaple) return name;` as its FIRST branch, above every pack rule; the screen passes `item.isUniversalStaple` (deliberately not the opted-in flag); a five-case guarded `describe` block covers it **including a control that fails if the flag goes inert.** Disabling the branch turned all five red: `1 container (26 oz) Kosher salt` where the ruling requires `Kosher salt`. **Live partition: 3,121 rows, 354 `isUniversalStaple`, 0 `isUserPantryStaple`, 19 `UNIVERSAL_STAPLES` members — it partitions cleanly.**
- ⚠️ **333 of the 354 staple rows STILL CARRY A STORED PACK that the render suppresses.** Not shopper-visible, but the data is still wrong underneath, and anything that changes the suppression surfaces it. **This is also why BUG-209 looked five times larger than it was.**

### BUG-172 — Narrow the merge-grouping key so different salts stay separate

- **Date:** August 27, 2026 · **Surface:** `groceryMerge.ts` · `groceryStaples.ts` · **Origin:** Hans's device ruling (item 8), *"iodized salt is NOT kosher is NOT flaky sea salt… salts are super different so keeping them separate is probably needed and best."*
- ✅ **SHIPPED `77dab10` (local, amended — see the ID note below).** Dropped from merge grouping: **the 10 salt entries + `black peppercorns`.** Kept: **the 6 ground-pepper spellings + all 5 olive-oil entries.**
- ⚠️ **THE TRAP WAS BIGGER THAN THE ONE CALL SITE, AND CC FOUND IT BEFORE EDITING.** `baseStapleName` has **four consumers.** Deleting the salt entries from `STAPLE_VARIANT_TO_BASE` as literally instructed would have **regressed PRD §2.2/§12.7 [LOCKED]** — kosher salt leaking onto the buy-list instead of greying — **and undone BUG-142, in one edit.** ⚠️ **Chat-Claude's instruction was wrong twice on this item: once on pepper (see BUG-168) and once here.**
- **The shape that shipped:** `STAPLE_VARIANT_TO_BASE` **unchanged**; a second, narrower `MERGE_GROUP_VARIANT_TO_BASE` answers the different question. ⚠️ **TWO MAPS OVER THE SAME INPUT DOMAIN IS DEFENSIBLE HERE — they answer "is this the same SUBSTANCE?" (flagging, conversion) versus "is this the same PURCHASE?" (merging) — AND THAT IS EXACTLY D-WS9-189's RELATIONSHIP 1 vs RELATIONSHIP 3.** ⚠️ **The residual risk is MEMBERSHIP DRIFT: a new variant (`pink himalayan salt`) added to one map and not the other. D-WS9-189 subsumes both — do not let a third map appear first.**
- ✅ **The BUG-142 guard held, and one break proved it:** C2 (removing the `groupConversion` fallback) went red — *"the variant pair must merge deterministically / 2 !== 1."* Measured `2026-08-27T21:28:04Z`: **552 → 552 rows, every `kosher salt` row byte-identical** — `11 tsp` on the August 27 device plan, `10.75 tsp` on the six August 26 clones. ⚠️ **The unchanged-between-runs guard is what made this valid; a hardcoded "11 tsp" would have FAILED FOR CORRECT CODE, since 11 and 10.75 are different plans, not a rounding.**
- **Only behavioural diff:** `ef8bfed5 | salt | 1 | pinch` emits later in the list — it no longer shares a group with `kosher salt`. **No quantity moved.**
⚠️ **ID COLLISION — RESOLVED August 28, 2026, AND THE RESOLUTION DEVIATES FROM THE PRECEDENT ON PURPOSE.** The three small-block commits were **already PUSHED** when the correction was attempted (verified three ways: `HEAD` == `origin/ws7-in-progress` == `77dab103`, all three SHAs `merge-base --is-ancestor` true, `rev-list --left-right --count` = `0 0`). **CC halted rather than rewriting published history — the pushed/unpushed gate in the correction prompt is what caught it.**
- **Canon's precedent is *code keeps the ID, the chat-side entry moves*** (BUG-143/BUG-145, August 25). ⚠️ **NOT APPLIED HERE, because that precedent assumes the ID is UNWRITTEN.** BUG-170 already had a cross-referenced entry in three docs, so moving it costs an entry rewrite plus a VOID stub, against **three comment lines.**

### BUG-174 — `bucketKeyOf` never normalises the unit, so `tsp` and `teaspoon` are two grocery rows

- **Date:** August 28, 2026 · **Surface:** `groceryList.ts:132` `bucketKeyOf(canonical, unit)` · **Found by:** CC, D-WS9-189 Phase 0 — **while asking what the relationship program must assert about units.**
- **Symptom:** the bucket key is `` `${normalizeIngredientName(canonical)}|${unit}` `` — **the name is normalised, the unit is not.** Two rows for one ingredient survive whenever the unit strings differ at all.
- ✅ **VERIFIED, TWO SHAPES, IGNORE-BLIND.** `rg --no-ignore --hidden "normalizeUnit"` across `groceryList.ts` · `groceryReconcile.ts` · `routes/groceryLists.ts` → **2 hits, both in `groceryListAI.ts`.** `find src -name "grocery*.ts" -exec grep -c` → `groceryList.ts` **0** · `groceryReconcile.ts` **0** · `groceryLists.ts` **0** · `groceryMerge.ts` 6 · `groceryListAI.ts` 2. **Zero in the bucketing path; zero at either persist site.** ⚠️ **And `normalizeUnit` is only `trim().toLowerCase()` — it would not merge `tsp`/`teaspoon` even where it IS applied.**
- **Measured on the 40 most recent lists: 31 groups where one `ingredientId` appears on more than one row. 29 of the 31 differ ONLY in unit. 19 of 40 lists carry at least one.** Live examples: `Whole milk 0.5 cup` ‖ `milk 1 each` (**14 lists**) · `kosher salt 0.875 tbsp` ‖ `Kosher salt 7.75 tsp` · `black pepper 3.625 tsp` ‖ `Black pepper 3 teaspoon` · `green cabbage 1 head` ‖ `green cabbage 3 cup` · `Salt 0.75 tsp` ‖ `Salt 1 pinch`.
- 🔴 **THIS IS A PREREQUISITE FOR D-WS9-189, NOT A FOLLOW-ON.** ⚠️ **`milk` and `Whole milk` ALREADY SHARE AN `ingredientId` and still render twice** — so perfect relationship data changes nothing for them. **A relationship is a statement about a PURCHASE, and a purchase has exactly one unit.** Either `bucketKeyOf` drops `unit` and a row carries a unit list, or a purchase-unit resolution stage runs before relationships are consulted.
- ⚠️ **IT ALSO BLOCKS BUG-137's GARLIC FOLD, WHICH HAS BEEN WAITING ON "UNIT NORMALISATION" SINCE AUGUST 24.** ⚠️ **ONE PREREQUISITE, TWO PROGRAMS — which makes it the highest-leverage unblocked item in the grocery arc.**
- **Also affects provenance:** the same key is the join used for `GroceryListItem` source rows, so a unit split fragments provenance too (see BUG-165).
⚠️ **AMENDED August 28, 2026, BUG-174 PHASE 0 (read-only, fresh CC chat) — THE HEADLINE FIGURE IN THIS ENTRY WAS WRONG AND CC REFUTED IT BY BREAKING IT OUT BY CAUSE.**
- ⚠️ **"29 of 31 duplicate groups differ ONLY in unit" IS REFUTED. EXACTLY ONE IS A SPELLING VARIANT.** The 29 counted groups whose **unit strings** differ — which is **not** the same as groups that differ only in unit **spelling**, and not the same as groups a spelling fix would merge. ⚠️ **The measurement never checked whether `canonicalName` ALSO differed, and for 14 of the 31 it does.** **A group whose names differ never enters the same merge group at all, so the unit is irrelevant to it.**
- **The real composition of the 31, measured by replaying the shipped `mergeConvertibleGroups` against all 31 real groups:**

| Cause | Groups | Class |
|---|---|---|
| Recurring synthetic `milk`/`limes` vs plan `whole milk`/`lime` — **different `canonicalName`, never grouped** | **14** | **not a unit problem — D-WS9-188** |
| `kosher salt` tbsp+tsp — **already merged by shipped code**; all 4 lists predate `dbd389e` | 4 | already fixed |
| volume↔volume, same name, no `gramsPerCup` — hot sauce ×2, ketchup, cilantro leaves | 4 | **BUG-176** |
| `teaspoon` + `tsp` — black pepper | **1** | **the only group (a)** |
| genuinely unconvertible — bunch+cup, pinch+tsp, cup+each, cup+head, cup+`large` | 8 | **correct as two rows** |

- 🔴 **CONSEQUENCE — THIS ENTRY'S "GATES TWO PROGRAMS" CLAIM IS HALF WRONG.** ⚠️ **BUG-174 DOES NOT UNBLOCK THE MILK CASE.** `milk 1 each` ‖ `Whole milk 3 tablespoon` is **12 of the 31, the largest single cause** — and it is a **NAME split, not a unit split.** They share an `ingredientId` only because persist re-resolves it from the final name; the merge groups on `canonicalName`. **The code already says so, at `groceryList.ts:516-519` verbatim: *"Whether a recurring 'milk' SHOULD absorb the plan's 'whole milk 3 tbsp' need is D-WS9-188 and is unruled."*** ⚠️ **The gate on those 14 is D-WS9-188 — a product ruling nobody has made — not this bug.**
- ✅ **WHAT SURVIVES, AND IT IS VERIFIED BY EXECUTION, NOT BY READING: BUG-137's GARLIC FOLD IS GENUINELY BLOCKED ON SPELLING NORMALISATION.** `childSet.size === 1` at `groceryMerge.ts:151` sees `{clove, cloves}` as two children and bails. Proven: `[head + clove]` → **1 row, merged**; `[clove + cloves]` → **2 rows, refused**; `[head + clove + cloves]` → **3 rows**. **Live data carries both (`clove` 961, `cloves` 16).** **This is the one place spelling normalisation alone unblocks a named program.**
- ✅ **THE ORPHAN RISK IS REFUTED — the bucket key is NEVER PERSISTED.** Two differently-shaped commands, one ignore-blind; `grep -rn -i "bucketkey\|sourceKey" schema.prisma` → **no hits**. Both join sites build and consume the keyspace **inside one request**.
- 🔴 **BUT TWO REAL HAZARDS WERE FOUND WHERE THE PROMPT DID NOT EXPECT THEM, AND THEY BOUND THE FIX:** (1) `groceryList.ts:299` **`signatureOfEffective` SHA-1s the RAW unit into `GroceryListItemSource.ingredientSignature`, which IS persisted** — normalising there invalidates every stored signature and forces a full re-resolve of every list. **Must not be touched.** (2) `groceryReconcile.ts:111-119` **`matchKey` compares a PERSISTED `GroceryListItem.unit` against a freshly consolidated unit** — distinct keyspace so no collision, but **if the fix rewrites the unit a row carries, a stored `teaspoon` row stops matching a fresh `tsp` row and reconciles as delete+add.** ⚠️ **THE RESULTING CONSTRAINT: CANONICALISE INSIDE THE KEY; NEVER REWRITE `item.unit`.**
- ⚠️ **AND THE FIX MUST REUSE, NOT ADD — THE ALIAS DATA ALREADY EXISTS THREE TIMES OVER.** `ingredientConversions.ts:62-117` already spells both `tsp` and `teaspoon` → 1/48; `prepCombineEngine.ts:148-229` already ships `UNIT_CANON` (41 keys) + `canonicalizeUnit()` + a family-aware `mergeBucketKey()` — **the exact semantics this bug wants, already tested, scoped to Cook Mode**; `stepAmountRefs.ts:137-152` has a private 50-entry map; the client mirrors it at `kiwi/lib/format/grocery.ts:377-394`. **A fifth map is the line-824 failure.** ⚠️ **Do NOT naively fold prep into grocery — they genuinely disagree (`pint` is count in one and volume in the other; `large` exists only in prep). See D-WS9-192.**
✅ **BUILT August 28, 2026 — `25cf718`, LOCAL, DEVICE PASS PENDING.** `canonicalUnitToken()` at `ingredientConversions.ts:201`, wired into `bucketKeyOf` **inside the key only**. ⚠️ **Derived at module load by grouping each factor table's keys by SHARED FACTOR — two keys with the same number ARE the same unit, and the number is the proof. No alias data added.** All three no-touch rulings verified **by diff, not by eye**: `signatureOfEffective` still raw at `groceryList.ts:317`, `matchKey` still raw at `groceryReconcile.ts:117`, and `git diff` against `groceryReconcile` · `prepCombineEngine` · `groceryListAI` source is **empty**.
- 🔴 **BUT BUG-137's GARLIC FOLD IS *NOT* UNBLOCKED, AND THAT WAS THIS BUG'S HEADLINE PAYOFF.** `clove`/`cloves` sit in **no factor table**, so `canonicalUnitToken` leaves them distinct and `childSet.size === 1` at `groceryMerge.ts:151` **still bails on `[clove + cloves]`.** ⚠️ **CC pinned the gap with an explicit test so it is visible rather than assumed — the right call.** **Unreached spelling families: `clove`/`cloves` (977 rows) · `can`/`cans` (89) · `stalk`/`stalks` (30) · `inch`/`inches` (26).**
- ⚠️ **CHAT-CLAUDE'S RULING 2 WAS WRONG AND CC NARROWED IT CORRECTLY.** The ruling named all three tables; CC excluded `COUNT_UNITS` because **it is a bare Set with no factor, so it proves nothing** — `whole`, `piece` and `ct` are members because they resolve grams via `gramsPerEach`, **which is a statement about density lookup, not about the words being spellings of each other.** Folding them would elect an arbitrary representative (the mechanical rule picks `ct`). ✅ **Corroborated by an untouched green assertion: `bucketKeyOf("paper towels", "each") === "paper towels|each"`.**
- **What BUG-174 actually delivers, measured at the correct stage:** **1 live duplicate group prevented** (`black pepper 3 teaspoon` ‖ `3.625 tsp` → one bucket) plus **forward protection on 27,939 of 35,734 dish-ingredient rows** now sitting in a folded family (tsp 11,202 · tbsp 7,709 · cup 5,316 · oz 2,060 · lb 1,652).
- ⚠️ **CC FOUND AND CORRECTED AN ATTRIBUTION FLAW IN ITS OWN HARNESS, WHICH IS THE MOST TRANSFERABLE THING IN THE REPORT.** The replay harness runs `mergeConvertibleGroups` over persisted rows, so it observes **the merge stage only** — **BUG-174 acts one stage earlier, at the bucket, and is invisible to it.** With BUG-176 stashed the harness reported 4 merges, implying BUG-174 resolved nothing. **An artefact of measuring at the wrong stage.** ⚠️ **And its first corrected probe said 3, also wrong — it used the `Ingredient` canonical for both rows of two lime groups, but one row is a recurring synthetic whose canonical is `limes`, so they never shared a bucket. Corrected to 1 before reporting.**
- **Seven deliberate breaks.** ⚠️ **Break 5 (`units.every` → `units.some`) STAYED GREEN and CC did not treat it as relief** — it grepped the mutation back (applied; all three files LF-only, so no CRLF no-op), then Break 6 showed the mirror image, and **Break 7 (both guards removed) reddened every cross-dimension assertion.** ✅ **Conclusion: two independently sufficient guards, so no single break can redden a merge-level assertion — the assertions are genuine guards and the redundancy is real.**
- ✅ **FOLLOW-THROUGH LANDED August 28, 2026 — `ad82955`, LOCAL. `COUNT_UNIT_ALIASES` at `ingredientConversions.ts:225`, four hand-written families, consulted AFTER the factor-derived election.** ⚠️ **THE MAP ALONE DID NOT CLOSE THE FOLD, AND CC PROVED THAT BY EXECUTION BEFORE ASSUMING IT** — `groceryMerge.ts:186-191` ran the sub-unit comparison on `normalizeUnit`, so both had to move. `[clove + cloves]` **2 rows REFUSED → 1 row `3 clove` MERGED**; `[head + clove + cloves]` **3 rows → 1 row `15 clove`**.
- 🔴 **CC CAUGHT ITSELF VIOLATING RULING 3 MID-BUILD, AND THIS IS THE MOST IMPORTANT LINE IN THE REPORT.** Its first working version **rewrote `item.unit`** — the sub-unit path sets `base.unit = child`, and `child` had become the canonical token, so `[head + cloves]` came out `12 clove` instead of `12 cloves`. ⚠️ **THAT RE-OPENS HAZARD 2: a stored `cloves` row would reconcile as delete+add.** **Only 2 live `GroceryListItem` rows carry `cloves` — and CC's own words are the right standard: *"small is not the standard."*** ✅ **Fixed at `groceryMerge.ts:202-207` — the canonical token decides WHICH ROWS SUM; the merged row then takes back a spelling that actually occurs in the group.** Pinned by a test; **Break 12 turns it red.**
- ⚠️ **THE RE-MEASURE MOVES NOTHING, AND CC REPORTED THAT RATHER THAN DRESSING IT UP.** 31 → 9 merge / 22 residual at the merge stage and 1 at the bucket stage — **identical to the previous block.** Cause measured, not assumed: **no `clove`/`cloves` pair exists among the 31, and the plan-level exposure query returns 0.** **This commit buys ZERO retrospective improvement.**
- ✅ **WHAT IT DOES BUY, MEASURED, AND IT IS FORWARD-LOOKING ONLY:** `clove`/`cloves` on garlic (953 + 16) · `can`/`cans` on chickpeas (7 + 1) · `stalk`/`stalks` on celery (29 + 1) · `inch`/`inches` on ginger (7 + 1). ⚠️ **Zero dishes carry both spellings WITHIN one dish, so the fold fires only when a plan combines a clove-spelling dish with a cloves-spelling one — which is why it is not device-observable today.**
- ✅ **NO FIFTH FAMILY, AND THE SCAN REPRODUCED THE SINGULARISER WARNING IN THIS MODULE'S OWN DATA:** `inches` fails strip-the-`s` (→ `inche`), **which is exactly why the four rows are hand-written rather than derived.** `heads` · `sprigs` · `leaves` · `bunches` · `slices` · `pinches` all have zero live rows and are **pinned as must-not-fold.**
- ✅ **THE `cloves` SPICE COLLISION WAS TESTED AGAINST REAL CATALOG ROWS, NOT A FIXTURE.** Two differently-shaped queries: 7 rows exist matching `%clove%`, including **`cloves` (Pantry, the spice)**, `ground cloves` (teaspoon ×17) and `whole cloves` (each ×11). **The map moves the UNIT half only** — `bucketKeyOf("ground cloves","teaspoon") === "ground cloves|tsp"`, name half still plural. **Break 11 (leaking the fold onto the name) reddens two of those tests.**
- **Five deliberate breaks, ALL RED.** ⚠️ **CC checked for the Break-5 redundancy rather than assuming its absence, and printed the map back in full after the "missing family" break instead of trusting an empty grep** — §27.3 discipline applied to its own break verification.
- ⚠️ **Three deliberate non-changes, each with a reason worth keeping:** `distinctUnits` (`:249`) **stays on `normalizeUnit`** — canonicalising it would collapse `{clove, cloves}` to one entry, take the early-out, and **silently defeat the very fold this block delivers**; `pickMeasuredTarget`'s `cup`/`cups` check left as redundant-but-harmless; `scalePurchaseForSubUnit` left alone (only `heads` would matter, zero live rows).
- ✅ **PROVEN END-TO-END ON A LIVE GENERATION, August 28, 2026 — AND AT THE BUCKET STAGE, WHICH IS THE ONLY THING THAT SETTLES IT.** One `dish_ingredient` row flipped `clove` → `cloves`, a grocery list generated through the **production route** (`createGroceryListsRouter()` with no deps, real AI, HTTP 200), then the row restored and both temporary lists deleted.

```
bucketKeyOf("garlic","clove")  = "garlic|clove"
bucketKeyOf("garlic","cloves") = "garlic|clove"      SAME KEY? true
pre-BUG-174 formula would have given: "garlic|clove" and "garlic|cloves"   -> TWO buckets before, ONE now
GARLIC AS RENDERED — 1 row:  "Garlic" 8 clove, pack "1 head", provenance 3 source rows   (8 = 2 + 2 + 4)
```

- ⚠️ **THE BUCKET KEY IS WHY THIS EXERCISE EXISTED. The merge-stage sub-unit path would ALSO have produced one row here, so the row count alone cannot distinguish a bucket-stage fold from a merge-stage one.** The counterfactual was computed from the pre-BUG-174 formula in a scratch script — **no source was modified to produce it.**
- 🔴 **CC'S FIRST RUN REPORTED "2 ROWS — THE FOLD DID NOT HOLD" AND THAT VERDICT WAS WRONG.** Its filter was `/garlic/i` on `displayName`, **which also matched `garlic powder` — a different ingredient in a different bucket.** It re-ran matching on `ingredientId` and added a line naming the near-miss explicitly. ⚠️ **THIRD SELF-CAUGHT MEASUREMENT ERROR IN THIS ARC, ALL THE SAME SHAPE — MEASURING THE WRONG THING AND READING IT AS A RESULT** (the merge-vs-bucket stage attribution; the `limes` canonical in the lime groups; now a name regex standing in for an identity). **A false NEGATIVE this time, which is the direction that would have blocked a correct push.**
- ✅ **Restore proven byte-equal against the record printed before the write, re-verified independently after the process exited.** Restore ran in a `finally`. **Plan returned to 0 grocery lists (was 0). `dish_ingredients` at `clove` 961 and at `cloves` 16 — both unchanged.**
- ✅ **CC declined to use the first plan it found** — the four newest each had an active list and the route 409s, so clearing one would have been a **second mutation.** It searched for a plan with **zero** non-archived lists instead, and picked an **August 13 draft** deliberately, not one Hans would device-test.
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED — pushed `77dab10..ad82955` August 29, 2026; the fold device-confirmed in the five-bug pass (garlic one row from several dishes, item 12) and the reconcile safety audit found zero rewritten rows across 357** · **Cross-ref:** **BUG-176** · **BUG-137** (still blocked) · **BUG-178** · D-WS9-192 · D-WS9-188.

### BUG-176 — `mergeGroup` routes every measured pair through grams, so `1 tbsp + 1 tsp` of one ingredient refuses to merge

- **Date:** August 28, 2026 · **Surface:** `groceryMerge.ts` `mergeGroup` → `ingredientConversions.ts:169-172` `convertToGrams` · **Found by:** CC, BUG-174 Phase 0, **arguing against its own prompt.**
- **Symptom:** two rows for one ingredient in two units of the SAME dimension — `hot sauce 1 tablespoon` ‖ `hot sauce 1 teaspoon` — ship as two rows. **`1 tbsp + 1 tsp` is 4 tsp. That is fixed kitchen arithmetic and needs no density.**
- ✅ **VERIFIED BY EXECUTION:** `isVolumeUnit('tablespoon')` = **true** · `isVolumeUnit('teaspoon')` = **true** · `convertToGrams(1,'tablespoon',null)` = **null**. ⚠️ **It returns null ONLY because `gramsPerCup` is missing — a per-ingredient datum that a volume→volume conversion does not need.** The merge then declines, `partitionForAI` rule 3 routes the pair to Sonnet, and the conservation guard refuses on the null.
- ⚠️ **THIS IS THE ROW HANS FLAGGED ON DEVICE AND CHAT-CLAUDE WRONGLY DEFENDED.** Device item 3, August 27: *"two rows next to each other both calling for '1 bottle (5 oz) hot sauce'"*. Chat-Claude ruled it **the designed failure mode working correctly** and recorded that in BUG-142's entry. **The guard did behave correctly given the path it was on — but the path was wrong, and it should never have reached the guard.** ⚠️ **Hans's read of his own product was right and the audit talked him out of it.**
- ✅ **THE FIX IS A REUSE, NOT A WEAKENING (§27.2).** A density-free same-dimension path **before** the grams path — volume↔volume and weight↔weight only. **`prepCombineEngine.ts`'s `VOLUME_RATIO`/`WEIGHT_RATIO` already encode exactly this**, and the client mirror states the principle outright: *"weight↔weight is the ONE cross-unit conversion that needs no per-ingredient data."*
- 🔴 **THE CONSERVATION GUARD IS NOT TOUCHED, AND THAT IS THE POINT.** It keeps refusing every case it was built for — `each`+`cup`, `pinch`+`tsp`, `bunch`+`cup` — because those cross dimensions and genuinely need a per-ingredient factor. **This change means those pairs are the only ones that ever reach it.**
- **Live population: 4 of the 31 duplicate groups** — hot sauce ×2, ketchup, fresh cilantro leaves. **The largest fixable bucket in the sample once the 14 name-splits (D-WS9-188) and 4 already-fixed are removed.**
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED — pushed `77dab10..ad82955` August 29, 2026; proven on device on TWO paths: fresh generation, and reconcile after an edit (hot sauce 1/2 cup + 1/4 cup → one 3/4-cup row)** · **Priority:** **P2** · **Cross-ref:** **BUG-174** · **BUG-142** (the guard this does NOT change) · BUG-170 · D-WS9-182.

### BUG-179 — The plan rationale claims cleanup savings from shared equipment on DIFFERENT nights, which saves nothing

- **Date:** August 29, 2026 · **Surface:** plan-rationale prompt body ("why this works") · **Found by:** Hans, device pass. ⚠️ **He caught a claim that is confidently stated, plausible-sounding, and literally false.**
- **The live text:** *"Meals 1 and 3 both use the sheet pan — same pan, similar oven temp, so cleanup is cut in half on those nights."*
- 🔴 **THE DEFECT: THE MEALS ARE ON DIFFERENT NIGHTS. You wash the pan after night 1.** Nothing is shared, no cleanup is halved, and *"similar oven temp"* across nights is meaningless — **each night preheats separately.**
- ✅ **HANS'S RULE, AND HE DREW THE LINE PRECISELY: the benefit of a repeated method is EASE PER NIGHT, NOT SHARED WORK ACROSS NIGHTS.** His words: *"Sheet pan meals on nights 1 and 3 DOES sound nice because half of the 4 meal week is an easy cooking method after the prep and assembly is done."* ⚠️ **And he explicitly does NOT want the claim banned — *"I don't want to prevent it from saying 'grill every night this week, keep the dishes to a minimum'"*. The method genuinely being low-dish is a real property. Sharing it across nights is not.**
- **What may be claimed / what may not:**

| Claim | Valid? | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Two dishes, **same night**, one oven temp | ✅ | One preheat, one cleanup. Genuinely shared. |
| A method that is **inherently** low-cleanup (sheet pan, one pot) | ✅ | A per-night property. *"Half your week is a one-pan night."* |
| **Prep** done once for several nights | ✅ | Real — that is Prep the Week. |
| **Ingredients** shared across meals | ✅ | Real — buy once, less waste. |
| Same **equipment** on different nights | ❌ | **You wash it in between.** |
| Same **oven temp** on different nights | ❌ | **Separate preheats.** |

- ⚠️ **AND A SECOND TRAP HANS NAMED: do not equate low-dish with low-effort.** *"a charcoal grill is more work than cleaning a plan."* **A method may cut dishes and still cost more total work — claim the dish count, not the effort.**
✅ **FIXED August 29, 2026 — `e502ba8`, LOCAL.** One shared `CLEANUP_CLAIM_RULES` const interpolated into **all three** generators, ⚠️ **as a single const rather than three copies, because three copies is the drift this block existed to prevent.** `aiPrompts.ts:205`'s taught example replaced by a per-night-method example. **`set_preferences`'s *"Sheet-pan and one-pot meals minimize cleanup midweek"* left alone AND PINNED BY A TEST, so a later pass cannot mistake it for the same defect.** ✅ **Versions read from the DB, not predicted: `set_preferences` v8→v9 · `directed` v5→v6 · `surprise` v3→v4. A second seed reported 0 bumps, confirming source and seeded bodies agree.** ✅ **CC also fixed `surprise`'s inline *"a shared sheet pan"* → *"a one-pan night"* — the hazard in miniature, and not in the instruction.**
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED — device-confirmed August 29, 2026 (items 5/rationale review: no false same-night claims), pushed `ad82955..bf63cf5`** · **Priority:** **P2** · ⚠️ **The de-falsified rationale is now merely vague — the content spec is BUG-190, not a reopen of this.** **Cross-ref:** **BUG-190** · D-WS9-190 · D-WS9-191.

- **The live rows, one plan (`d0d1bea8`), verbatim from the export:**

```
olive oil                3 tablespoon   1 bottle (17 oz)     ← Spicy Arrabbiata
extra virgin olive oil   5 tablespoon   1 bottle (25.5 oz)   ← Salmon | Cherry Tomatoes | Garlic Spinach
extra-virgin olive oil   5 tablespoon   1 bottle (25.5 oz)   ← Sheet-Pan Chicken | Smashed Potatoes
```

- 🔴 **THE LAST TWO DIFFER BY ONE HYPHEN. 13 tablespoons of olive oil, ordered as THREE BOTTLES.**
- ⚠️ **AND THIS IS NOT A GAP IN THE DATA — `mergeGroupBaseName` CARRIES A HARDCODED OLIVE-OIL FAMILY MAP AND IT DID NOT FIRE.** ⚠️ **UNVERIFIED HYPOTHESIS, to be settled by reading the map: either the hyphen variant is absent from it, or `normalizeIngredientName` does not strip hyphens, or the map is not consulted on this path.** **Do not build from any of the three until one is proven.**
- ✅ **WHY IT MATTERS BEYOND ITSELF: this is the exact class D-WS9-189 exists to replace, caught while the hardcoded map is still the mechanism.** **Three catalog rows for one shopper product, and the family map that was supposed to cover precisely this family.**
⚠️ **PHASE 0, August 29, 2026 — CHAT-CLAUDE'S DIAGNOSIS REFUTED. THE FAMILY MAP IS CORRECT AND FOLDS ALL THREE NAMES.** `MERGE_GROUP_VARIANT_TO_BASE` lives at **`groceryStaples.ts:196-224`** (not `groceryMerge.ts:180-206` as cited) and contains **`extra-virgin olive oil` AND `extra virgin olive oil`, both → `olive oil`.** ⚠️ **The hyphen is irrelevant. `normalizeIngredientName` does not touch hyphens, but it does not need to — the map folds both spellings by name.**
- ✅ **THE REAL CULPRIT, NAMED AT THE STAGE: `distinctUnits.size < 2` — the SAME-UNIT EARLY-OUT at `groceryMerge.ts:267-271`.** All three rows **do** reach one merge group (the fold works), and the group is then **pushed through unmerged because every member carries the same unit**. **`mergeGroup` is never called.**
- 🔴 **`mergeConvertibleGroups` IS A CROSS-UNIT RECONCILER AND HAS NEVER MERGED SAME-UNIT ROWS.** Proven by execution: identical three names at **the same unit → 3 rows out**; the same three at **differing units → 1 row out.** ⚠️ **So `MERGE_GROUP_VARIANT_TO_BASE` is effectively DEAD for the commonest case there is — same product, same unit — which is precisely Hans's list.**
- ✅ **AND THE BLAST RADIUS IS TIGHT, WHICH IS WHAT MAKES THE FIX SAFE:** `bucketKeyOf` already canonicalises **both** name and unit, so two rows sharing a canonical and a unit are **already one bucket**. **Same-unit multiples inside a merge group can therefore only arise from the fold — so enabling same-unit merge touches only the 11 folded family members and nothing else in the catalog.**
- ⚠️ **A SECOND, SEPARATE DEFECT WAS FOUND AND IS NOT PART OF THIS FIX — SEE BUG-184.** `extra virgin olive oil` (**66 dish refs**) and `extra-virgin olive oil` (**403**) are **two distinct `Ingredient` rows with no alias linking them.** **The code fix closes BUG-181 without it; the duplicate row is D-WS9-189 authoring territory.**
✅ **FIXED August 29, 2026 — `8feb87e`, LOCAL, DEVICE PASS PENDING.** The `distinctUnits` precondition dropped from the early-out (only the arity test survives) and a same-unit branch added **first** in `mergeGroup`, ahead of every conversion path. **Live, on plan `d0d1bea8`, reported by stage: 3 olive-oil buckets in → ONE row out, `13 tablespoon` from 6 sources** (3 + 5 + 5, matching the export exactly). **The bucket stage is unchanged and still splits three ways — that was never the defect.** ✅ **Regression as a relationship: all 49 live lists replayed, 2,879 rows in, 2,868 → 2,826 out. 42 rows collapsed, and EVERY distinct name in the diff is a fold-map family member (3 olive oil spellings, 3 ground-pepper spellings). 2,793 rows byte-identical. Named guards row-for-row identical: kosher salt (46), flaky sea salt (7), hot sauce (12), garlic (44), ketchup (18).**
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED — device-confirmed August 29, 2026 (item 1: one olive-oil row, 13 tablespoons, one bottle; item 2: ground-pepper family folds, peppercorns separate), pushed `ad82955..bf63cf5`** · **Priority:** **P2** · **Cross-ref:** **BUG-137** · **D-WS9-189** · D-WS9-192 · BUG-174.

D does not, because it names the staples that genuinely are staples instead of deriving them.**

### BUG-190 — Plan-rationale bullets assert non-advantages: co-occurrence claimed as savings, with no quantity grounding — ✅ **HANS-SPEC'D, and the spec is the acceptance test**

- **Date:** August 29, 2026 · **Surface:** the plan-rationale prompt body — the same body BUG-179 just versioned · **Found by:** Hans, five-bug device pass, items 5/6, reading a live rationale
- **What he saw:** *"asparagus in two meals"* claimed as an advantage — only true if both meals each need a fraction of a bunch — and *"3 vegetables can be bought in one farmer's market trip,"* which is not an advantage at all. **BUG-179's fix held (no false same-night-equipment claims); what survives is vague and unpersuasive.** His verdict: *"the advantages its saying aren't applicable or real."*
- ✅ **HANS'S SPEC, VERBATIM:** *"This section is intended to tell the user 1: how the meal works together to reduce wasted or leftover ingredients like 'two mexican nights share the same fresh cilantro, 3 peppers split across two nights,' or more broadly 'vegetable portions shared across multiple meals to reduce throwaway' and 2. how it meets their unique prefernces (weekly pacing, eating style, avoidances/allergies, cook time), and especially stuff they wrote instead of selected. and Bonus 3. how prepping ahead can make the cooking less than 15 minutes per night with this plan (if that's true or applicable. or like 'prep ahead and have dinner on the table in under 20 minutes 3 of 5 nights')"*
- ⚠️ **THE PRINCIPLE UNDER THE SPEC: CO-OCCURRENCE IS NOT AN ADVANTAGE.** A sharing claim is real only when grounded in quantities — two meals drawing on one purchased pack. The same shopper-reality test that reframed BUG-188 and the D-WS9-189 rubric: **what leaves the store, not what appears twice.**
- 🔴 **PHASE 0 QUESTION THAT DECIDES THE BUILD SHAPE: does the rationale prompt's input carry ingredient quantities and pack data?** If yes, this is prompt wording (§7 loop: edit `aiPrompts.ts`, reseed, version bump; check the cache boundary before editing above it). **If no, it is a loader change first** — and the free-text preference passthrough needs the same existence check for claim (2). Claim (3)'s prep-ahead timing needs the prep-sequencing output visible to the prompt; same check.
- ~~⚠️ §7 footgun status inherited from BUG-179's diagnosis: this body is seeded and carries no cache marker — a reseed works on this prompt.~~ ⚠️ **CACHE CLAIM CORRECTED BY PHASE 0 (August 31): the bodies DO carry a cache marker** — `{{storeShortlist}}` (`wizard.ts:137-141`), and every rationale section sits on the CACHED side of it. Editing the wording forces a one-time re-cache on the streaming build-plans path — an accepted cost, not a defect; the cache-split guard test asserts prefix-vs-tail invariance, not frozen bytes, so it survives the edit. `directed`/`surprise` are uncached.
- 🔴 **PHASE 0 RESULT (August 31) — THE GATE HIT: (a) is NO, STRUCTURALLY.** There is no standalone rationale prompt — the bullets are the `whyBullets` field of the THREE plan generators (`set_preferences` / `directed` / `surprise`, sharing `CLEANUP_CLAIM_RULES`), authored at CANDIDATE stage, **before any ingredient exists** (the model is still inventing `mealTitles`; the shelf shape is deliberately ingredient-free, and `whyBullets` is never re-derived at expand or finalize — `wizardExpansion.ts:401`, `wizardFinalize.ts:462`). ⚠️ **"Asparagus in two meals" is not a wording lapse — it is the only kind of claim the stage can make.** The body already carries Hans-shaped quantity examples and the model degrades them to co-occurrence because it has no quantities to check against. (b) free-text: full on build-plans; `additionalNotes` missing from `directed` + `surprise` inputs. (c) prep output: absent by construction (no plan exists yet).
- ✅ **ROUTE RULED (chat-Claude, delegated, August 31 — objection window open): Route 1+, and intents 1 & 3 move to D-WS9-191.** Now: rewrite the shared rationale sections to BAN sharing/waste claims at candidate stage (ungroundable there) and lead with **preference fit, free-text first** — intent 2, fully supported today — fewer, true bullets; plus the small loader touch passing `additionalNotes` into the `directed` and `surprise` generate inputs so intent 2 works on all three paths. **Intents 1 (quantity-grounded sharing) and 3 (prep-ahead timing) are ASSIGNED TO THE D-WS9-191 SPEC** — that redesign already owns the choose-time surface, Hans has already accepted 2–3s more latency there, and its Phase 0 already found the cheap partial (DB-read descriptions for store-bound slots). Building a second rationale pipeline ad hoc would be the 4b-4 build-it-twice mistake.
- **Status:** ✅ **DEVICE-CONFIRMED September 1, 2026 — 10 of 10 PASS, zero banned shapes on any of the three paths. LOCAL, PUSH PENDING.** Hans on item 1: *"it passes, and does a big focus on the user. which is what I asked for."* Item 5, the question that justified the pass: the three candidates' bullets were *"varied enough to be worth reading or tie breaking"* — **which is the surface's whole purpose and the thing a checklist could not have told us.** ⚠️ **THE THIN-BULLET RISK IS REAL AND MEASURED, NOT HYPOTHETICAL: item 4 came back at TWO bullets per card on a sparse preference profile, and Hans's own diagnosis is the mechanism — *"I didn't specify a lot of uniqueness in my preferences, and the more I put the better the text bullets were."* Bullet quality is now a FUNCTION OF PREFERENCE RICHNESS, so the emptiest profile — a brand-new user's — gets the weakest bullets on the screen where they first judge the product.** **He passed it for this block; it is a live D-WS9-191 input, not a closed question.** **Two follow-ups minted from the same pass: BUG-195 (invented tags) and D-WS9-200 (the food-waste spec).** Prior status: 🟡 BUILT, LOCAL, UNPUSHED — device read pending. Two commits `622085b` (prompt rewrite + guard test) · `5de3e56` (smoke script). A shared **`WHY_BULLETS_RULES`** const now serves all three generators — the one-place-no-drift idiom `CLEANUP_CLAIM_RULES` already uses — replacing each body's rationale section **in place** (§10). It bans sharing/waste claims *and states the structural reason in the body*, leads free-text-first, permits ONE honest bullet over three padded ones, and names both of Hans's live bullets as banned shapes verbatim. Version bumps diff-driven and clean: set_preferences v9→v11 · directed v6→v8 · surprise v4→v6 (two seed runs, exactly 3 bumps each, no other key moved). Suites 1757/0. Live output on v11, zero banned shapes: *"No mushrooms anywhere — you said they're a hard no in your house"* · *"Chosen to come together in about 30 minutes, the cap you set"* · *"Mostly one-pan or skillet nights so you can keep a hand free while cooking with a toddler underfoot."* ✅ **Chat-Claude verified independently against the working tree:** the const is defined once and interpolated at all three sites, and `CLEANUP_CLAIM_RULES`'s prohibitions are intact.
- ✅ **THE WASTE HEURISTIC SURVIVES WHERE IT BELONGS — as MENU COMPOSITION, not as speech.** A new `# Menu composition — waste` section keeps the model *choosing* overlapping ingredients, sealed off from `whyBullets` by an explicit line. **We banned saying it, not doing it — plan quality does not regress.**
- ✅ **CC AMENDED `CLEANUP_CLAIM_RULES` AGAINST AN EXPLICIT "KEEP IT EXACTLY AS-IS" AND WAS RIGHT — ACCEPTED (chat-Claude, verified in file).** It removed two *Allowed* bullets ("Ingredients shared across meals — buy once, less waste" · "Prep genuinely done once and used across several nights") because **they authorize, in the same rendered prompt, precisely the claim this bug bans** — the §10 two-instructions-one-topic failure, with the model free to pick. **Removing an allowance only tightens; BUG-179's guard is its PROHIBITIONS, its same-night case and its dish-count rule, and all are byte-identical with its five test cases green.** ⚠️ **Twelfth consecutive block in which CC refuted a chat-Claude instruction and was correct.**
- 🔴 **THE AUTHORIZED LOADER TOUCH WAS IMPOSSIBLE, AND THE ERROR WAS CHAT-CLAUDE'S.** The go-ahead told CC to pass `additionalNotes` into the `directed` and `surprise` inputs "mirroring build-plans." **There is nothing to mirror: `additionalNotes` exists ONLY in `WizardInputSchema`, has no `UserPreferences` column, and `wizard.tsx:441` is its sole origin.** Those paths do not DROP it — **they have nothing to pass**, and already carry every free-text field that exists for them (`userInput`/`dietaryNotes`/`pickyAvoidances` for directed; `dietaryNotes`/`pickyAvoidances` for surprise). ⚠️ **Phase 0 stated the fact correctly and framed it as a "gap"; chat-Claude relayed the framing into an authorization without checking the field existed on those paths — the relay-without-verification shape again (§27.5).** CC correctly made no loader change and instead documented per-path free-text inventories in the Input sections (tail-side of the cache marker, so free), deleting `surprise`'s actively misleading *"There is no user free-text"* line.
- ✅ **CC CAUGHT ITS OWN OVER-CLAIM AND RESEEDED:** its first example, *"Every dinner lands under 30 minutes"*, asserted a ceiling the same body calls a soft unverifiable bias — **the bug's own defect class, in the fix.** Now: *"claim that you AIMED at it, never that you verified it; you are choosing titles here, not timing recipes."*
- **Disclosed, accepted:** three live generations rather than one ($0.154 total; one was an operator mis-page), and the smoke script committed rather than left in scratch, per the repo's `ws9-bug*` convention.
- **Priority:** **P2** · **Cross-ref:** BUG-179 · BUG-159/BUG-163 (where descriptive text actually persuades) · **D-WS9-191 (owns intents 1 & 3)**.

### BUG-200 — The grocery list shows garlic twice: a head AND cloves, as two separate buy lines

- **Date:** September 3, 2026 · **Surface:** grocery list · **Found by:** Hans, device pass, while checking the pantry-staple need lines (BUG-171).
- **Symptom:** a plan needing garlic emits **both** a head-of-garlic line and a separate garlic-cloves line. The shopper buys one thing; the list asks for two.
- ⚠️ **THIS IS THE HEAD-vs-UNIT CLASS, NOT A SPELLING DUPLICATE.** `head` and `clove` are different units of the same purchase — exactly what the four-label relationship model exists to express. **Do not fix it one-off with a name fold:** a suffix or spelling fold cannot express *"a head contains cloves"*, and D-WS9-189 explicitly forbids one-off fragmentation fixes.
- **Owner:** **D-WS9-189**, the ingredient-relationship program. ⚠️ **It is a `COMPONENT` relationship (a clove is a component of a head), which the reader folds only if it takes DIRECT edges — D-WS9-201.**
- ⚠️ **EARLIER GREEN DOES NOT COVER THIS.** A `[head + clove]` garlic case was measured as already passing *before* the August 31 block, and CC found zero dishes carrying both spellings. **This instance is a different shape from the one that was checked** — do not read that pass as coverage.
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED — D-WS9-189 A3, September 6, 2026.** **`beba57d`** (A3 — hash confirmed from the repo's own reflog on September 7, 2026, not from a report). **Closed through the `subUnit` ladder, NOT the component pool** — the hand map folds `garlic head` / `head of garlic` / `whole garlic head` / `garlic cloves` / `fresh garlic` into `garlic`, routing the family into `mergeGroup`'s subUnit branch, and a new guard drops the resulting parent-is-its-own-child slot. **All three live head+clove lists now emit ONE garlic row:** `2 heads Garlic (14 clove)` + `11 garlic cloves (11 each)` → **`3 heads Garlic (25 cloves)`** · `3 heads` + `2 heads head of garlic` → **`5 heads Garlic (50 cloves)`** · `1 head` + `2 heads head of garlic` → **`3 heads Garlic (29 cloves)`**. ⚠️ **25 cloves → 3 heads is D-WS9-220's arithmetic exactly.** · **Cross-ref:** **D-WS9-189** · D-WS9-201 · D-WS9-205 · BUG-171 · BUG-123.

### BUG-201 — 🔴 An empty allergy array DISARMS the retrieval filter entirely, and one failed preferences request is enough to send one

- **Date:** September 4, 2026 · **Surface:** `wizard.tsx` / `tellkiwi.tsx` → `wizard.directed.generate` → `buildStoreShortlist` · **Found by:** Lane A Phase 0, **demonstrated against the live database with Prisma query logging** — not a code read.
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED — September 4, 2026, pushed. DEVICE-CONFIRMED BY HANS the same day** (device pass v3 item 6: Gluten-free returns no pizza/pasta; Dairy-free reaches coconut curries). **Commits: `2bbf3cb` (client half) + `6a82218` (server half)** — stamped September 6, 2026 from `git log`, matched by commit subject. · **Priority:** 🔴 **P1** · **Cross-ref:** **D-WS9-211** · D-WS9-206 · D-WS9-077 · D-WS9-078.
- ⚠️ **BOTH HALVES WERE REQUIRED AND THEY SHIPPED IN SEPARATE COMMITS.** The client half stopped sending an empty array; the server half stopped treating an empty array as "no filter". **With only one landed the defect survives** — that is why this entry closes on two commits, not one.

**⚠️ THE SERVER HALF IS PROVEN END-TO-END.** Two arms run for a real user (stored `allergiesAndAvoidances: ["Fish-free"]`), SQL diffed:

- **ARM 1** (client sends the real chips) — the WHERE carries **both** predicates: `(NOT "meals"."allergens" && $7)` **and** `"meals"."allergens" <> '{}'`.
- **ARM 2** (client sends `[]`) — **both predicates are ABSENT from the SQL. Not weakened. Gone.**
- **Consequence on the actual shelf:** of the 40 meals ARM 2 put in front of the model, **1 carries the token this user's chip excludes** (*Blackened Salmon…* `["dairy","fish"]`) plus **3 unstamped meals that fail-closed would also have blocked.**

**🔴 REACHABILITY IS WORSE THAN ASSUMED.** `_layout.tsx:38-45` sets **`retry: false`** on the QueryClient and `getPreferences` throws on any non-2xx. **There is no retry buffer — a single failed `/me/preferences` request puts the wizard into ARM 2.** The screen then renders the "Allergies & avoidances" expander with **zero chips selected, indistinguishable from a user who set none.**

- ⚠️ **UNVERIFIED HYPOTHESIS — the CLIENT half.** That the app actually sends `[]` on a prefs error is still a code read (`wizard.tsx:94, :181, :211-215`; same shape in `tellkiwi.tsx`) because Lane A was forbidden to edit `artifacts/kiwi/` to instrument it. **The defect is real either way:** the server must not disarm its own safety filter on an empty array, whatever the client's reason for sending one.
- 🔴 **THE FIX IS NOT "TREAT EMPTY AS UNSET" — THAT AMBIGUITY IS THE BUG.** A user with genuinely no allergies also sends `[]`, so the array alone cannot distinguish *"none"* from *"hydration failed."*

**✅ RULED BY HANS, September 4, 2026 — DISTINGUISH OMITTED FROM EMPTY.** ⚠️ **He first said "option (a), union with stored" and then described behaviour that was option (b); the description was what he meant, and pushing back on the mismatch is what settled it.** His words: *"it should be whatever was sent from that screen — the user sets their defaults and then they can override/change at the last minute if they want,"* and on confirmation: *"if the wizard prefs show NO allergies (nothing selected, text is null) it tells the wizard no allergies. that's what I want."*

- **The contract:** field **OMITTED** → the screen never loaded preferences → **server resolves from stored**. Field **PRESENT, including empty** → the user's actual choice on that screen → **server honours it exactly**.
- 🔴 **THE DISCRIMINATOR IS WHETHER THE SCREEN LOADED, NOT WHETHER IT IS EMPTY.** Loaded-and-empty is a deliberate "no allergies" and must be honoured. Never-loaded is *unknown* and must fall back. **The client must not send an allergy value it never successfully hydrated** — that sentence is the whole fix.
- **Why union was rejected:** it can never drop a stored allergy, but it also breaks the case Hans built the field for — *"maybe a user has a mild gluten thing and they want to cook whatever tastes better for a party this weekend. that's ok."* ⚠️ **A per-run override the user can SEE but cannot actually exercise is worse than not offering one.**
- ⚠️ **Chat-Claude's registered caveat:** this fix trusts the client to be honest about its own load state. Mitigation is observability, not another gate — **log when a generate arrives with no allergen field for a user who HAS stored allergies.**
- ⚠️ **AND THE UI MUST STOP LYING ON FAILURE.** Today a failed hydration renders the expander with zero chips — indistinguishable from a user who set none. **If the screen could not load preferences it must say so**, or the user cannot exercise an override whose starting state they cannot see.
- **Fix location:** server contract + the client's send-nothing-on-failure behaviour. **Bundle with the `allergensStampedAt` follow-on** — both are server-side, both gated on the EPERM clearing.

### BUG-203 — 🔴 Clearing a free-text preference to empty does not persist, and the UI reports success

- **Date:** September 4, 2026 · **Surface:** Profile → Preferences → "Anything else?" (`dietaryNotes`) → `PATCH /me/preferences` · **Found by:** Hans, device pass item 7.
- **Status:** ✅ **FIXED — September 4, 2026, pushed. DEVICE-CONFIRMED BY HANS the same day** (device pass v3 items 1 and 2: the free-text note clears and stays cleared; stovetop type also clears — **a second live instance of the same bug that nobody had reported**). **Commit: `2bbf3cb`** — stamped September 6, 2026 from `git log`, matched by commit subject. · **Priority:** **P2** · **Cross-ref:** **D-WS9-206** · D-WS9-207 · BUG-201.
- **Symptom, verbatim:** *"I cleared out a note from before 'no cilantro, light dairy' and it didn't take. it toasted on the preferences, but maybe there's an issue with saving it to null from something."*
- 🔴 **THE TOAST IS THE WORST PART.** The screen reports a successful save for a change that was never sent. A silent no-op that announces success is worse than a visible failure.
- **UNVERIFIED HYPOTHESIS, but it has a named basis:** Lane B's own audit recorded that `dietaryNotes` carries **three different blank-value conventions** across the four screens — `undefined` / `""`-plus-omit-on-PATCH / `undefined` — and concluded they *"converge to the same stored state."* ⚠️ **THE DEVICE FALSIFIED THAT.** An omit-on-blank convention means an emptied field is **dropped from the PATCH body**, so the server never receives it and the stored value survives. **Clearing is exactly the case where "omit if empty" and "save empty" diverge, and it is the one case the audit's convergence claim could not have covered.**
- ⚠️ **CHECK EVERY FREE-TEXT AND ARRAY FIELD ON THAT PATCH, NOT JUST `dietaryNotes`.** The convention is shared; if one field cannot be cleared, the others probably cannot either. **Emptying a field is a distinct test case from changing it** — the suite has no coverage for it (the extracted component's tests assert `onChangeText` wiring, not the clear-to-empty round trip).
- ⚠️ **This is the same class as BUG-201, one layer up:** an empty value being treated as *"no value supplied"* rather than *"the user supplied nothing on purpose."* **Fix them with the same distinction in mind.**
**✅ ROOT CAUSE FOUND — AND IT IS NOT WHERE THE HYPOTHESIS PUT IT.** Not omit-on-blank in the PATCH builder. **`preferences.tsx` mapped an emptied field to `undefined` in the FORM BUFFER (`v.length > 0 ? v : undefined`), and `JSON.stringify` silently deletes `undefined` keys** (`client.ts:143`). Server never saw the field → `.optional()` read it as *not supplied* → old note survived → **200 → success toast.** ⚠️ **The hypothesis was right about the outcome and wrong about the location; the shape of the guess pointed at the PATCH layer and the defect was one layer earlier.**

- ✅ **THE FIX IS THE INVARIANT, NOT THE FIELD.** A new `toPatchBody()` in `preferencesForm.ts`, mirroring the `toFormState()` it sits beside, **guarantees the body carries no `undefined` values at all — so no future field can be dropped this way.**
- ✅ **AND THE TOAST WAS FIXED BY MAKING IT TRUE, NOT BY MAKING IT CONDITIONAL.** CC's framing, and it is the better answer than the one asked for: *"The PATCH now carries the change, so a 200 means the change landed. Making it conditional would have been treating the symptom."*
- 🔴 **A SECOND LIVE INSTANCE NOBODY REPORTED: `stovetopType`.** It has a deselect affordance (`StovetopPicker:24`), the same silent no-op, and the same success toast. **Found only because the sweep was required across every field rather than the one Hans hit** — §27.3 paying rent immediately. `cookingSkill` and `defaultRetailer` were latent (select-only, no clear affordance); `maxCookTimeMinutes` was already correct; the 8 array fields serialize `[]` correctly and are now pinned.
- ⚠️ **SELF-CAUGHT REGRESSION IN THE FIRST FIX, and it is a cross-feature trap worth remembering:** the initial version sent `""`. The server stamps `dietaryUpdatedAt` on a dietary change by comparing `string | null` (**BUG-055**), so `""` vs stored `null` counts as a change — **every unrelated preference edit by a note-less user would have marked all their plans dietarily stale.** Empty now collapses to `null`.

- ✅ **Device item 8 is NOT a second bug and should not be logged as one.** Hans saw the wizard revert his per-run edit to the stored note; that is the designed per-run behaviour working correctly. It looked wrong only because BUG-203 had prevented the stored value from being cleared in the first place.
